Chaal ke baad

    4.6 Calculation 1: sochne ka routine aur 1 chaal
    ================================================

6-step routine, 1 chaal ki calculation, Calculation Gym

Yaad rakho

-   Har position mein ek hi routine: Dhamki → Target → Candidates → Line →
    Ruko → Blunder check.
-   Candidates ka order: pehle checks, phir captures, phir threats (CCT).
-   1 chaal ki calculation: meri chaal ke baad opponent kya le sakta hai, kya
    check de sakta hai?
-   Pehli achhi chaal dikhe to bhi blunder check karo.
-   Calculation Gym mein roz 10-15 puzzles: pehle sahi, phir tez.

Is lesson ke topics

-   4.6.1 Calculation = dimaag mein chaalein khelna
-   4.6.2 6-step routine (har puzzle, har game)
-   4.6.3 Sawal 1: Routine ka pehla step kya hai?
-   4.6.4 Sawal 2: Candidates kis order mein dekhte hain?
-   4.6.5 1 chaal ki calculation: 3 sawal
-   4.6.6 Abhyaas: Routine lagao
-   4.6.7 Abhyaas: Target dhundo
-   4.6.8 Abhyaas: Captures bhi dekho
-   4.6.9 Sawal 3: Tumhe ek achhi chaal mil gayi
-   4.6.10 Ab roz ki practice: Calculation Gym

    4.6.1 Calculation = dimaag mein chaalein khelna
    -----------------------------------------------

-   Calculation ka matlab hai board ko chhuye bina aage ki chaalein sochna:
    meri chaal, opponent ka best jawab, phir meri chaal.
-   Do cheezein chahiye:
    -   Thorough (poora): koi zaroori jawab chhoote nahi.
    -   Fast (tez): faltu lines par time barbaad na ho.
-   Yeh talent nahi, routine aur practice hai.
-   Is course mein calculation 1 chaal se shuru hoti hai aur dheere-dheere 8
    chaal tak jaati hai.
-   Har step chhota hai, isliye koi bhi seekh sakta hai.

    4.6.2 6-step routine (har puzzle, har game)
    -------------------------------------------

-   1.
-   Dhamki: opponent ki pichli chaal kya chahti hai?
-   Mere kis piece par khatra hai?
-   2.
-   Target: kaunse pieces bina protection hain?
-   King kitna khula hai?
-   Kaun do kaam kar raha hai?
-   3.
-   Candidates: 2-3 chaalein chuno.
-   Pehle checks, phir captures, phir threats.
-   4.
-   Line: ek candidate ko end tak socho.
-   Har chaal par opponent ka sabse forcing jawab lo.
-   5.
-   Ruko: jab koi check, capture ya threat na bache, ruk jao aur material
    gino.
-   6.
-   Blunder check: chalne se pehle dekho, opponent ke paas koi check, capture
    ya threat to nahi bacha.
-   Shuru mein yeh slow lagega. 100-200 puzzles ke baad yeh apne aap 10-20
    second mein hone lagta hai.

    4.6.3 Sawal 1: Routine ka pehla step kya hai?
    ---------------------------------------------

Routine ka pehla step kya hai?

Jawab:

✓ Opponent ki dhamki dekhna

-   Pehle bachao.
-   Opponent ki pichli chaal kya karna chahti hai, yeh na dekha to apni jeet
    ki line bhi haar mein badal sakti hai.

    4.6.4 Sawal 2: Candidates kis order mein dekhte hain?
    -----------------------------------------------------

Candidates kis order mein dekhte hain?

Jawab:

✓ Checks → Captures → Threats

-   Checks ke jawab sabse kam hote hain, isliye unhe calculate karna sabse
    aasaan aur tez hai.
-   Phir captures, phir threats.

    4.6.5 1 chaal ki calculation: 3 sawal
    -------------------------------------

-   Har candidate chaal par khud se 3 sawal pucho:
-   1.
-   Kya yeh check, capture ya threat hai?
-   2.
-   Is chaal ke baad opponent kya le sakta hai aur kya check de sakta hai?
-   3.
-   Jo piece maine chalaya, kya woh safe hai?
-   Aur jo piece pehle use bacha raha tha, ab kaun bachayega?
-   Teeno ka jawab theek ho, tabhi chalo.

    4.6.6 Abhyaas: Routine lagao
    ----------------------------

Routine lagao: checks pehle. 1 chaal mein mate.

    4 | . . . P . P P .
    5 | . . . . . p . .
    6 | . . n p . . . .
    7 | p p p . . . p p
    8 | r . b k q b r .
       ----------------
        h g f e d c b a

-   Qd1#: d-file khuli thi aur White king ke paas koi square nahi.

    1 | R N K . q B Q R

    3 | . P . . . P P .

Chaal ke baad

    4.6.9 Sawal 3: Tumhe ek achhi chaal mil gayi
    --------------------------------------------

Tumhe ek achhi chaal mil gayi. Ab kya?

Jawab:

✓ Blunder check: opponent ke checks, captures, threats

-   Routine ka aakhri step.
-   Lasker: achhi chaal dikhe to ruko, behtar dhundo, aur pakka karo ki koi
    jaal nahi.

    4.6.10 Ab roz ki practice: Calculation Gym
    ------------------------------------------

-   Calculation Gym course ka apna section hai: course list mein is level ke
    turant baad, aur har level page par us level ke Gym steps.
-   Usmein 8 depth hain (1 se 8 chaal).
-   Har depth ke 6 steps hain: Asaan → Medium → Andhe (board chhupa) → Mushkil
    → Poori line → GM.
-   Har step mein aakhri 10 mein 8 sahi aur target time ke andar, tab agla
    step khulta hai.
-   Galat puzzle 3 puzzles baad dobara aata hai, aur review mein bhi.
-   Mushkil lage to coach mode apne aap chalu hota hai (routine + hint).
-   Roz 10-15 puzzles.
-   Jitni practice lage, utni karo.

    4.7 Openings ka naksha: families aur repertoire
    ===============================================

4 families, gambit, system, aur openings seekhne ki 5 seedhiyan

Yaad rakho

-   1.e4 e5 = Open game. 1.e4 + koi aur jawab = Semi-open. 1.d4 d5 = Closed.
    Baaki (c4, Nf3, ...Nf6 + ...g6) = Indian aur flank.
-   Repertoire = 3 tayyar jawab: White ke liye ek opening, Black ke liye 1.e4
    ke khilaaf ek, 1.d4 ke khilaaf ek.
-   Naam se pehle idea: har opening chaal center, development ya king safety
    ke liye hoti hai.
-   Gambit = pawn (ya piece) de kar tez development ya attack. System =
    opponent kuch bhi khele, same setup.
-   Anjaan opening mile to ghabrao mat: 10 principles se khelo.

Is lesson ke topics

-   4.7.1 Har opening 4 families mein se ek
-   4.7.2 Sawal 1: Yeh kaunsi family hai?
-   4.7.3 Sawal 2: Black ne 1...c5 khela
-   4.7.4 Sawal 3: Kaunsi family?
-   4.7.5 Sawal 4: Kaunsi family?
-   4.7.6 Sawal 5: White ne 1.c4 khela
-   4.7.7 Repertoire: tumhare 3 tayyar jawab
-   4.7.8 Sawal 6: Ek beginner ka repertoire kitne hisse ka hota hai?

-   4.7.11 Sawal 8: "System" opening ka sabse bada fayda?
-   4.7.12 Har opening ki 5 seedhiyan
-   4.7.13 Sawal 9: Opponent ne aisi opening khel di jo tumne kabhi nahi dekhi

    4.7.1 Har opening 4 families mein se ek
    ---------------------------------------

-   Duniya mein hazaaron opening naam hain, par sab 4 families mein aate hain:
    -   Open game (1.e4 e5): pieces jaldi bahar, tez khel. Italian, Ruy Lopez,
        Scotch.
    -   Semi-open (1.e4 aur Black ...e5 nahi khelta): Sicilian, French,
        Caro-Kann, Scandinavian.
    -   Closed (1.d4 d5): dheema, plan wala khel. Queen's Gambit, London.
    -   Indian aur flank (1.d4 Nf6, 1.c4, 1.Nf3): center ko door se control.
        King's Indian, Nimzo-Indian, English.
-   Family pata ho to plan bhi aadha pata hota hai.

    8 | r n b q k b n r
    7 | p p p p p p p p
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | P P P P P P P P
    1 | R N B Q K B N R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    4.7.7 Repertoire: tumhare 3 tayyar jawab
    ----------------------------------------

-   Har opening yaad karna zaroori nahi.
-   Tumhe sirf apna repertoire chahiye:
    -   White ke liye 1 opening (jaise 1.e4 aur Italian Game)
    -   Black: 1.e4 ke khilaaf 1 jawab (jaise 1...e5, ya Caro-Kann)
    -   Black: 1.d4 ke khilaaf 1 jawab (jaise Queen's Gambit Declined)
-   Baaki openings ki sirf pehli chaalein aur idea jaano, taaki opponent kuch
    bhi khele, tum ghabrao nahi.
-   Yeh lesson series wahi sikhaati hai.

    4.7.8 Sawal 6: Ek beginner ka repertoire kitne hisse ka hota hai?
    -----------------------------------------------------------------

Ek beginner ka repertoire kitne hisse ka hota hai?

Jawab:

✓ 3: White, Black vs 1.e4, Black vs 1.d4

-   Teen tayyar jawab kaafi hain.
-   Baaki openings ka bas idea.

    4.7.9 Gambit aur system kya hote hain?
    --------------------------------------

-   Gambit: opening mein jaan-boojh kar pawn (kabhi piece) dena, taaki badle
    mein tez development, center ya attack mile.
-   Yahan 2.f4 King's Gambit hai: White f4 pawn deta hai taaki e5 hate aur
    center khule.
-   System: ek setup jo opponent kuch bhi khele, lagbhag same rehta hai.
-   Jaise London System: d4, Bf4, e3, Nf3, c3, Bd3.
-   Kam theory, par opponent ko pata ho to woh sahi plan se jawab de sakta
    hai.
-   Is series mein har gambit aur har system ke saath uska defence bhi
    sikhoge.

    8 | r n b q k b n r
    7 | p p p p . p p p
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . p . . .
    4 | . . . . P P . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | P P P P . . P P
    1 | R N B Q K B N R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    4.7.10 Sawal 7: King's Gambit (2.f4) mein White pawn kyun deta hai?
    -------------------------------------------------------------------

King's Gambit (2.f4) mein White pawn kyun deta hai?

Jawab:

✓ Center aur tez development ke liye

-   Gambit = material ke badle samay (development) aur center.

    4.7.11 Sawal 8: "System" opening ka sabse bada fayda?
    -----------------------------------------------------

"System" opening ka sabse bada fayda?

Jawab:

✓ Kam theory: same setup har baar

-   System kam yaad karna padta hai.
-   Nuksaan: opponent ko tayyar jawab pata ho sakta hai.

    4.7.12 Har opening ki 5 seedhiyan
    ---------------------------------

-   Har opening is course mein 5 level mein aayegi:
-   1.
-   Beginner (abhi): pehli 4-8 chaal, har chaal kyun, aur 1 trap.
-   2.
-   Club: main line 10 chaal tak, opponent ki galat sidelines ko saza.
-   3.
-   Advanced: 12-15+ chaal ka tree, move orders aur transpositions, pawn
    structure aur middlegame plan.
-   4.
-   Master: model games, critical lines, typical endgames.
-   5.
-   Mastery: apni repertoire file, roz ka review, apni games se update.
-   Abhi sirf seedhi 1.
-   Baaki seedhiyan tactics, plans aur endgames seekhne ke baad aayengi.

    4.7.13 Sawal 9: Opponent ne aisi opening khel di jo tumne kabhi nahi dekhi
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Opponent ne aisi opening khel di jo tumne kabhi nahi dekhi. Kya karoge?

Jawab:

✓ 10 principles se khelo: center, development, castle

-   Principles har opening mein kaam karte hain.
-   Naam pata hona zaroori nahi.

    4.8 Open games: pehli chaalein (1.e4 e5)
    ========================================

Italian, Ruy Lopez, Scotch, Petrov, Philidor, Vienna, King's Gambit

Yaad rakho

-   1.e4 e5 2.Nf3 Nc6 ke baad teesri chaal naam deti hai: 3.Bc4 Italian, 3.Bb5
    Ruy Lopez, 3.d4 Scotch.
-   Italian: bishop c4 kamzor f7 ko dekhta hai. Plan: c3, d3, castle.
-   Ruy Lopez: bishop b5 e5 ke defender (Nc6) par dabaav. 3...a6 4.Ba4 bishop
    rakho.
-   Petrov 2...Nf6: 3.Nxe5 ke baad 3...d6! pehle. 3...Nxe4? par 4.Qe2!.
-   Ajeeb jawab (2...f5, 2...d5) ka jawab: seedha development aur center,
    laalach nahi.

Is lesson ke topics

-   4.8.1 Sabse purani family
-   4.8.2 Italian Game (Giuoco Piano)
-   4.8.3 Abhyaas: Italian Game
-   4.8.4 Ruy Lopez (Spanish)
-   4.8.5 Sawal 1: Ruy Lopez mein White ka bishop b5 asal mein kisko dabaata
    hai?
-   4.8.6 Scotch Game
-   4.8.7 Petrov Defence: copy mat karo!
-   4.8.8 Abhyaas: Black ne galti se copy kiya
-   4.8.9 Philidor Defence
-   4.8.10 Vienna Game
-   4.8.11 King's Gambit: accept aur develop
-   4.8.12 Abhyaas: Black ne ajeeb 2...f5?
-   4.8.13 Abhyaas: Elephant Gambit (2...d5?!) mein Black ne 3...e4 khela,
    knight par…
-   4.8.14 Sawal 2: Yeh kaunsi opening hai?

    4.8.1 Sabse purani family
    -------------------------

-   1.e4 e5 2.Nf3 (knight e5 par attack) 2...Nc6 (pawn bachao) sabse common
    shuruaat hai.
-   Ab White ki teesri chaal opening ka naam tay karti hai:
    -   3.Bc4 Italian Game
    -   3.Bb5 Ruy Lopez (Spanish)
    -   3.d4 Scotch Game
    -   3.Nc3 (...Nf6 ke saath) Four Knights

    4.8.2 Italian Game (Giuoco Piano)
    ---------------------------------

-   Beginner ke liye sabse achhi opening: seedhe plans.

1. e4 e5 2. Nf3 Nc6 3. Bc4 Bc5 4. c3 Nf6 5. d3 d6 6. O-O O-O

-   1.e4 Center aur bishop/queen ke liye raasta.
-   2.Nf3 Knight develop, e5 par attack.
-   2...Nc6 Pawn bachaya.
-   3.Bc4 Bishop f7 ko dekhta hai: shuru mein f7 ko sirf king bachata hai.
-   3...Bc5 Black bhi f2 ko dekhta hai.
-   4.c3 Idea: baad mein d4 se center.
-   5.d3 Shaant aur solid: e4 bacha.
-   6.O-O Castle! King safe.

    8 | r . b q k . n r
    7 | p p p p . p p p
    6 | . . n . . . . .
    5 | . . b . p . . .
    4 | . . B . P . . .

    4.8.4 Ruy Lopez (Spanish)
    -------------------------

-   Top players ki favourite.
-   Dheere-dheere dabaav.

1. e4 e5 2. Nf3 Nc6 3. Bb5 a6 4. Ba4 Nf6 5. O-O Be7 6. Re1 b5 7. Bb3 d6 8. c3
O-O

-   3.Bb5 Bishop e5 ke defender Nc6 par dabaav.
-   3...a6 Bishop se pucho: kya karoge?
-   4.Ba4 Bishop rakha (Morphy Defence).
-   5.O-O Pehle castle, e4 ki chinta baad mein.
-   6.Re1 Rook e4 ko bachata hai.
-   7.Bb3 Bishop phir bhi f7 ki line par.
-   d4 ki taiyaari.

    8 | r . b q k b n r

    4.8.6 Scotch Game
    -----------------

-   White turant d4 se center kholta hai.

1. e4 e5 2. Nf3 Nc6 3. d4 exd4 4. Nxd4 Bc5 5. Be3 Qf6 6. c3 Nge7

-   3.d4 Seedha center par chot.
-   4.Nxd4 Knight center mein, pawn wapas.
-   4...Bc5 Knight d4 par dabaav.
-   5.Be3 Knight bachaya, bishop develop.
-   6.c3 Knight ko aur sahara.

    4.8.7 Petrov Defence: copy mat karo!
    ------------------------------------

-   Black 2...Nf6 se ulta e4 par attack karta hai.
-   Solid opening, par ek zaroori trick yaad rakho.

1. e4 e5 2. Nf3 Nf6 3. Nxe5 d6 4. Nf3 Nxe4 5. d4 d5 6. Bd3 Nc6 7. O-O Be7

-   2...Nf6 Petrov: Black bhi attack karta hai.
-   3...d6 Pehle knight ko bhagao! 3...Nxe4? galat hai.
-   4...Nxe4 Ab pawn wapas lo.

    1 | R . B K Q B N R

    4.8.9 Philidor Defence
    ----------------------

-   2...d6: Black e5 ko pawn se bachata hai.
-   Solid par thoda passive.

1. e4 e5 2. Nf3 d6 3. d4 Nf6 4. Nc3 Nbd7 5. Bc4 Be7 6. O-O O-O

-   e5 bacha, par bishop f8 thoda band.
-   e4 par counter-attack.
-   e5 ko phir se bachaya.

    4.8.10 Vienna Game
    ------------------

-   2.Nc3: pehle knight, phir f4 ka idea.

1. e4 e5 2. Nc3 Nf6 3. f4 d5 4. fxe5 Nxe4 5. Nf3 Be7

-   2.Nc3 Knight d5 aur e4 dekhta hai.
-   e5 par chot: attacking idea.
-   3...d5 Black ka sahi jawab: center mein counter.
-   5.Nf3 Develop karo, d4 ki taiyaari.

    8 | r n b q k . . r
    7 | p p p . b p p p
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . p P . . .
    4 | . . . . n . . .
    3 | . . N . . N . .
    2 | P P P P . . P P
    1 | R . B Q K B . R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

Final position (5…Be7)

    4.8.11 King's Gambit: accept aur develop
    ----------------------------------------

-   2.f4 pawn deta hai.
-   Black pawn le sakta hai, par phir jaldi development aur center mein
    counter zaroori.

1. e4 e5 2. f4 exf4 3. Nf3 d5 4. exd5 Nf6 5. Bc4 Nxd5

-   2.f4 Gambit: e5 ko hatao, f-file kholo.
-   2...exf4 Gambit accept.
-   ...Qh4+ rokta hai aur develop.
-   3...d5 Center mein counter! Pawn wapas dene ko bhi tayyar.
-   4...Nf6 Develop karo, d5 wapas loge.

    1 | R . . K Q B N R
    2 | P P . . P P P P
    3 | . . N . . . . .
    4 | . . p . . B . .
    5 | . . . . n . . .
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    7 | p p p . . p p p
    8 | r . b k q b n r
       ----------------
        h g f e d c b a

Final position (5…Nxd5)

    4.8.12 Abhyaas: Black ne ajeeb 2...f5?
    --------------------------------------

Black ne ajeeb 2...f5? (Latvian Gambit) khela, White ne e5 le liya, aur Black
ne 3...Qf6 se knight par attack kiya. White: knight ko support do aur center
lo.

    8 | r n b . k b n r
    7 | p p p p . . p p
    6 | . . . . . q . .
    5 | . . . . N p . .
    4 | . . . . P . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | P P P P . P P P
    1 | R N B Q K B . R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

(White ki chaal)

Jawab:

1. d4

Ye bhi sahi: Nc4

-   4.d4! knight ko sahara aur center.
-   Latvian gambit apne king ko kamzor karta hai, White seedha development se
    aage rehta hai.

    8 | r n b . k b n r
    7 | p p p p . . p p
    6 | . . . . . q . .
    5 | . . . . N p . .
    4 | . . . P P . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | P P P . . P P P
    1 | R N B Q K B . R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    4.9 Sicilian: pehli chaalein (1.e4 c5)
    ======================================

Open Sicilian, Najdorf, Dragon, Sveshnikov, Taimanov, Alapin, Smith-Morra,
Grand Prix

Yaad rakho

-   1...c5 se Black d4 ko side se rokta hai. Board ek-tarfa (asymmetric): jeet
    ke chances dono taraf.
-   Open Sicilian: 2.Nf3 + 3.d4, cxd4, Nxd4. Black ko c-file aur center ka
    extra pawn milta hai.
-   Black ki 2nd/5th chaal family batati hai: ...a6 Najdorf, ...g6 Dragon,
    ...e5 Sveshnikov (Nc6 + Nf6 ke saath), ...e6 + ...Nc6 Taimanov.
-   Anti-Sicilian: 2.c3 Alapin (...Nf6 ya ...d5), 2.d4 cxd4 3.c3 Smith-Morra
    (lo aur jaldi develop, ya ...Nf6 se mana), 2.Nc3 + f4 Grand Prix.
-   Open Sicilian mein Nxd4 ...Nf6 ke baad Nc3 se e4 bachao.

Is lesson ke topics

-   4.9.1 Sicilian ka idea
-   4.9.2 Open Sicilian: Najdorf
-   4.9.3 Abhyaas: Black ka knight e4 par attack kar raha hai
-   4.9.4 Dragon
-   4.9.5 Sveshnikov
-   4.9.6 Taimanov
-   4.9.7 Alapin (2.c3) aur Black ka jawab
-   4.9.8 Smith-Morra Gambit: lena aur bachna
-   4.9.9 Smith-Morra: mana karna (decline)
-   4.9.10 Grand Prix Attack
-   4.9.11 Sawal 1: Yeh kaunsa Sicilian hai?
-   4.9.12 Sawal 2: Open Sicilian mein ...cxd4 trade se Black ko kya milta
    hai?

    4.9.1 Sicilian ka idea
    ----------------------

-   1...c5 duniya mein 1.e4 ka sabse popular jawab hai.
-   Black d4 ko side se control karta hai.
-   Baad mein ...cxd4 ke badle Black ko c-file (aadhi khuli) aur center mein
    ek extra pawn milta hai.
-   Position ek-tarfa hoti hai: jo achha khele woh jeete. Draw kam.

    8 | r n b q k b n r
    7 | p p . p p p p p
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . p . . . . .
    4 | . . . . P . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | P P P P . P P P

    4.9.12 Sawal 2: Open Sicilian mein ...cxd4 trade se Black ko kya milta hai?
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Open Sicilian mein ...cxd4 trade se Black ko kya milta hai?

Jawab:

✓ Aadhi khuli c-file aur center mein extra pawn

-   Black ka d/e pawn bachta hai (2 center pawns vs White ka 1), aur c-file
    rooks ke liye.

    4.10 French, Caro-Kann, Scandinavian aur baaki
    ==============================================

1.e4 ke solid aur ajeeb jawab: unka idea aur White ka plan

Yaad rakho

-   French 1...e6 2.d4 d5: solid. Kamzori: bishop c8 andar band. Advance
    (3.e5) ke khilaaf ...c5!.
-   Caro-Kann 1...c6 2.d4 d5: French jaisa solid, par bishop c8 pehle bahar
    (...Bf5/...Bg4), phir ...e6.
-   Scandinavian 1...d5: 2.exd5 Qxd5 3.Nc3 tempo ke saath queen ko bhagao.
-   Pirc/Modern (...d6, ...g6) aur Alekhine (1...Nf6): White ko center dete
    hain, baad mein chot karte hain. White: bada center + develop.
-   Caro-Kann trap: ...Nd7 aur Qe2 ke baad ...Ngf6?? par Nd6#.

Is lesson ke topics

-   4.10.1 French Defence: Advance Variation
-   4.10.2 Abhyaas: French Advance

    4.10.1 French Defence: Advance Variation
    ----------------------------------------

-   1...e6 aur 2...d5.
-   White 3.e5 se jagah leta hai.
-   Black ka sabse zaroori jawab: ...c5 se pawn chain ki jad (d4) par chot.

1. e4 e6 2. d4 d5 3. e5 c5 4. c3 Nc6 5. Nf3 Qb6

-   d5 ki taiyaari.
-   2...d5 Center mein chot.
-   3.e5 Advance: jagah lo.
-   3...c5 Pawn chain ki jad d4 par chot!
-   d4 par aur dabaav.
-   d4 aur b2 dono par.

    4.10.9 Pirc aur Modern
    ----------------------

-   Black ...d6, ...Nf6, ...g6 se center White ko deta hai.
-   White: bada center aur seedha development (Be3, Qd2, f3).

1. e4 d6 2. d4 Nf6 3. Nc3 g6 4. Be3 Bg7 5. Qd2 c6 6. f3 b5

-   e4 mazboot, g4/h4 ka attack plan.

    4.11 1.d4 d5: Queen's Gambit aur uske jawab
    ===========================================

QGD, QGA, Slav, Albin Countergambit

Yaad rakho

-   Queen's Gambit 1.d4 d5 2.c4: asli gambit nahi, White pawn aksar wapas le
    leta hai.
-   QGD 2...e6: sabse solid. Plan: ...Nf6, ...Be7, castle, baad mein ...c5 ya
    ...e5.
-   QGA 2...dxc4: lo, par pawn pakad ke mat rakho. ...b5? par a4 aur Qf3 jaisi
    tricks.
-   Slav 2...c6: d5 bachao aur bishop c8 bahar rakho.
-   Albin 2...e5: tricky. White e3?? mat khele: Lasker trap mein ...fxg1=N+!.

Is lesson ke topics

-   4.11.1 Queen's Gambit ka idea
-   4.11.2 Queen's Gambit Declined
-   4.11.3 Queen's Gambit Accepted
-   4.11.4 Abhyaas: Black ne laalach mein pawn ...b5 se pakda
-   4.11.5 Slav Defence
-   4.11.6 Albin Countergambit
-   4.11.7 Abhyaas: Albin mein White ne 4.e3?
-   4.11.8 Sawal 1: Queen's Gambit Accepted mein Black ko sabse zyada kya nahi
    karna…
-   4.11.9 Sawal 2: Yeh kaunsa jawab hai?

    4.11.1 Queen's Gambit ka idea
    -----------------------------

-   2.c4 White ka c pawn Black ke d5 (center) pawn ko hatane ki koshish hai.
-   Naam "gambit" hai, par Black pawn le to bhi White aksar e3 + Bxc4 se wapas
    le leta hai.
-   Black ke teen main jawab:
    -   2...e6 Declined (QGD)
    -   2...dxc4 Accepted (QGA)
    -   2...c6 Slav

    8 | r n b q k b n r
    7 | p p p . p p p p
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . p . . . .
    4 | . . P P . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | P P . . P P P P
    1 | R N B Q K B N R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    4.12 Indian defences aur flank openings
    =======================================

King's Indian, Nimzo, Queen's Indian, Grunfeld, Benoni, Dutch, English, Reti,
KIA, Bird

Yaad rakho

-   Indian = 1.d4 Nf6: knight pehle, pawns baad mein. Bishop aksar fianchetto
    (g7/b7).
-   King's Indian (...g6, ...Bg7, ...d6, ...e5): White ko center do, phir
    kingside attack. Grunfeld (...g6 + ...d5): center par turant chot.
-   Nimzo (3.Nc3 Bb4): knight par pin, e4 rokna. Queen's Indian (3.Nf3 b6):
    bishop b7/a6.
-   Dutch 1...f5: e4 square ko control, kingside attack. Benoni ...c5 + ...e6:
    queenside majority.
-   Flank: English 1.c4, Reti 1.Nf3, KIA (d3, Nf3, g3, Bg2), Bird 1.f4. From
    Gambit trap: Nc3?? par ...Qh4+.

Is lesson ke topics

-   4.12.1 King's Indian Defence
-   4.12.2 Grunfeld
-   4.12.3 Nimzo-Indian
-   4.12.4 Queen's Indian
-   4.12.5 Benoni

-   4.12.12 Sawal 2: Yeh kaunsi opening hai?

    attack.

    4.13 Gambits: lena hai ya mana karna hai?
    =========================================

King's Gambit, Evans, Danish, Blackmar-Diemer, Budapest, Englund, Stafford

Yaad rakho

-   Gambit ke 3 jawab: (1) lo aur jaldi develop karo, (2) lo aur sahi waqt par
    wapas do, (3) mana karo aur solid center chaal khelo.
-   Pawn lene ke baad bhi principles: develop, castle, center. Laalach mein
    queen se pawn mat bhaago.
-   King's Gambit decline 2...Bc5: 3.fxe5?? par 3...Qh4+ rook jeetta hai.
-   Budapest mein axb4?? par ...Nd3#. Englund (1.d4 e5?!) mein Bc3?? par
    ...Qc1#.
-   Stafford (3...Nc6) ke khilaaf: 4.Nxc6 dxc6 5.d3 seedha aur safe.

Is lesson ke topics

-   4.13.1 Gambit se kaise niptein
-   4.13.2 Abhyaas: King's Gambit Declined
-   4.13.3 Evans Gambit: lo aur wapas do
-   4.13.4 Danish Gambit: pawn wapas do
-   4.13.5 Blackmar-Diemer Gambit
-   4.13.6 Abhyaas: Budapest Gambit
-   4.13.7 Abhyaas: Englund Gambit (1...e5?!) ka trap
-   4.13.8 Abhyaas: Stafford Gambit
-   4.13.9 Sawal 1: Gambit lene ke baad sabse badi galti kya hai?
-   4.13.10 Sawal 2: Yeh kaunsa gambit hai?

    4.13.1 Gambit se kaise niptein
    ------------------------------

-   Gambit tumhe pawn deta hai aur badle mein samay chahta hai.
-   Tumhare 3 raaste:
-   1.
-   Lo aur jaldi develop karo. Pawn lo, par phir pieces bahar, castle.
-   2.
-   Lo aur wapas do. Jab pawn bachana mushkil ho, use wapas de kar apne pieces
    active karo.
-   3.
-   Mana karo (decline). Ek solid center chaal ya develop karte hue jawab.
-   Galat raasta: pawn ko bachane ke liye queen ya pawns se baar-baar
    chaalein, aur development bhool jaana.

    4.13.2 Abhyaas: King's Gambit Declined
    --------------------------------------

King's Gambit Declined: Black ne 2...Bc5 khela aur White ne laalach mein
3.fxe5?? le liya. Black, saza do!

    1 | R N B K Q B N R
    2 | P P . . P P P P
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . P . . . .
    5 | . . . P . b . .
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    7 | p p p . p p p p

        h g f e d c b a

(Black ki chaal)

Jawab:

1… Qh4+

-   3...Qh4+! 4.g3 Qxe4+ aur h1 rook gaya, ya 4.Ke2 Qxe4#.
-   Isliye 2...Bc5 ke baad White 3.Nf3 khelta hai.

    1 | R N B K Q B N R
    2 | P P . . P P P P
    3 | . . . . . . . .

    4.13.3 Evans Gambit: lo aur wapas do
    ------------------------------------

-   Italian mein 4.b4.
-   Black pawn leta hai aur ...d6 se jaldi develop karta hai.

1. e4 e5 2. Nf3 Nc6 3. Bc4 Bc5 4. b4 Bxb4 5. c3 Ba5 6. d4 d6

-   4.b4 Evans Gambit.
-   5.c3 Tempo ke saath bishop par attack, d4 ki taiyaari.
-   6...d6 Pawn wapas dene ko tayyar, pieces bahar.

    1 | R . . K Q B N R

    4.13.4 Danish Gambit: pawn wapas do
    -----------------------------------

-   White do pawns deta hai!
-   Sabse aasaan jawab: ...d5 se ek pawn wapas aur free development.

1. e4 e5 2. d4 exd4 3. c3 d5 4. exd5 Qxd5 5. cxd4 Nc6 6. Nf3 Bg4

-   3.c3 Danish.
-   3...d5 Center mein counter, pawn wapas.
-   d4 par attack, develop karte hue.

-   Trick ki koshish (...Bc5, ...Ng4) ke saamne seedha development.

-   Pieces bahar laao.

    4.14 Systems: London, Colle, Stonewall aur unke khilaaf
    =======================================================

System ka setup, aur Black ka seedha plan

Yaad rakho

-   London: d4, Bf4, e3, Nf3, c3, Nbd2, Bd3. Bishop pawn chain ke bahar.
-   London ke khilaaf: ...c5 aur ...Qb6 se b2 aur d4 par chot.
-   Colle: d4, Nf3, e3, Bd3, c3, Nbd2, phir e4. Black: bishop c8 jaldi bahar
    (...Bg4/...Bf5).
-   Stonewall: d4, e3, f4, Bd3. e5 square kamzor; Black ...b6 + ...Ba6 se
    achha bishop trade kare.
-   Systems mein kam theory, par plan ek jaisa: opponent ka plan jaano.

Is lesson ke topics

-   4.14.1 London System
-   4.14.2 London ke khilaaf: ...c5 aur ...Qb6
-   4.14.3 Abhyaas: London ke khilaaf Black ka plan
-   4.14.4 Colle System
-   4.14.5 Stonewall Attack
-   4.14.6 Trompowsky (2.Bg5)
-   4.14.7 Sawal 1: London System ke khilaaf Black ka sabse seedha plan?
-   4.14.8 Sawal 2: System openings ka nuksaan kya hai?

    4.14.1 London System
    --------------------

-   Club players ki favourite.
-   Ek setup, har jawab ke khilaaf.

1. d4 d5 2. Bf4 Nf6 3. e3 e6 4. Nf3 c5 5. c3 Nc6 6. Nbd2 Bd6 7. Bg3 O-O 8. Bd3

-   2.Bf4 Bishop pehle bahar, e3 se pehle.
-   d4 mazboot.
-   7.Bg3 Bishop trade se bachao.

    8 | r n b q k b . r
    7 | p p . . . p p p
    6 | . . . . p n . .
    5 | . . p p . . . .

    3 | . . P . P N . .

    4.14.2 London ke khilaaf: ...c5 aur ...Qb6
    ------------------------------------------

-   Black ka seedha aur strong plan: ...c5 se center par chot, ...Qb6 se b2
    par (bishop f4 ab b2 ko nahi bachata).

1. d4 d5 2. Bf4 c5 3. e3 Nc6 4. c3 Qb6 5. Qb3 c4 6. Qc2 Nf6

-   2...c5 Turant chot.
-   b2 par attack.
-   5...c4 Queen ko bhagao, queenside par jagah.
-   6...Nf6 Develop. Black ko queenside par jagah mili, plan ...b5-b4.

    1 | R N B K Q . N R
    2 | P P P P . P P P
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . B . P . . .
    5 | . . . . p p . .
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    7 | p p p p . . p p

    4.14.4 Colle System
    -------------------

-   Bishop d3, phir e4 se center kholo.

8. dxc5 Bxc5 9. e4

-   9.e4 Colle ka asli idea: e4 break.

Final position (9.e4)

    4.14.5 Stonewall Attack
    -----------------------

-   d4, e3, f4, Bd3: kingside attack ka setup.
-   Kamzori: e4 aur e5 squares.
-   Black sahi khele (jaise yahan bishop g4 jaldi bahar) to Black ko aaram
    milta hai, isliye iska defence zaroor jaano.

1. d4 d5 2. e3 Nf6 3. Bd3 c5 4. c3 Nc6 5. f4 Bg4 6. Nf3 e6 7. O-O Bd6

-   5.f4 Stonewall: pawns c3-d4-e3-f4.
-   5...Bg4 Bishop bahar, chain ke baahar.

    8 | r . b q k b . r
    7 | p p . . p p p p
    6 | . . n . . n . .
    5 | . . p p . . . .
    4 | . . . P . P . .
    3 | . . P B P . . .
    2 | P P . . . . P P
    1 | R N B Q K . N R

        a b c d e f g h

    4.14.6 Trompowsky (2.Bg5)
    -------------------------

-   1.d4 Nf6 2.Bg5: knight par chot.
-   Black ...Ne4 se bishop ko bhagata hai.

1. d4 Nf6 2. Bg5 Ne4 3. Bf4 c5 4. f3 Qa5+ 5. c3 Nf6

-   2.Bg5 Trompowsky.
-   2...Ne4 Bishop ko pucho.
-   3...c5 Center par chot.
-   4...Qa5+ Check se b2/c3 kamzor.

    1 | R N B K Q . N R
    2 | P P . P . . P P
    3 | . . P . . P . .
    4 | . . B . P . . .
    5 | . . . . . p . q
    6 | . . n . . . . .
    7 | p p p p p . p p
    8 | r . b k . b n r
       ----------------
        h g f e d c b a

Final position (5…Nf6)

    4.14.7 Sawal 1: London System ke khilaaf Black ka sabse seedha plan?
    --------------------------------------------------------------------

London System ke khilaaf Black ka sabse seedha plan?

Jawab:

✓ ...c5 aur ...Qb6

-   Bishop f4 par jaane ke baad b2 ko koi nahi bachata.
-   ...c5 + ...Qb6 turant sawaal poochhta hai.

    4.14.8 Sawal 2: System openings ka nuksaan kya hai?
    ---------------------------------------------------

System openings ka nuksaan kya hai?

Jawab:

✓ Opponent ko plan pata ho to tayyar jawab de sakta hai

-   Same setup = predictable.
-   Isliye har system ke khilaaf ek tayyar plan rakho.

    4.15 Practice: openings pehchano aur chalao
    ===========================================

Mixed sawaal: naam, idea aur agli chaal

Yaad rakho

-   Pehli 2-3 chaal se family aur opening ka naam pehchano.
-   Agli chaal dhundo: develop + kisi cheez par nazar (center, attack,
    defence).
-   Trap dekho: opponent ki pichhli chaal ne kya khula chhoda?

✓ Smith-Morra Gambit

-   2.d4 cxd4 3.c3 = Smith-Morra Gambit.

    ######################################################################
    ##  SECTION: 🧠 CALCULATION GYM: 1 SE 8 CHAAL TAK
    ######################################################################

Course ka section: Level 4 ke baad shuru, phir har level ke saath kuch steps.

-   Gym mein 47 steps hain, 8 depth (kitni apni chaalein: 1 se 8+). Har depth
    ke steps: Asaan (Board dikhega, coach saath), Medium (Board dikhega),
    Andhe (Dekho, yaad karo, phir board chhup jaata hai), Mushkil (Board
    dikhega, zyada mushkil), Poori line (Board nahi hilta: poori line dimaag
    mein), GM (Sabse mushkil, poori line dimaag mein). Depth 1 mein "Poori
    line" nahi.
-   Step pass: kam se kam 20-55 puzzles, aakhri 10 mein 8 bina hint/jhaank ke
    sahi (GM step par 9), aur sahi waalon ka median time target ke andar.
-   Pehle rules, phir practice: har depth tabhi khulti hai jab uska rules
    lesson ho gaya. Puzzle mein sirf woh themes aate hain jinka lesson ho
    chuka.
-   Level test se pehle Gym ladder us level ke step tak pass honi chahiye
    (neeche table).
-   Roz 10-15 puzzles: pehle sahi, phir tez. Galat puzzle 3 puzzle baad dobara
    aata hai aur review mein bhi jaata hai.

    Har puzzle ka routine (6 steps)
    ===============================

1.  Dhamki: opponent ki pichli chaal kya karna chahti hai? Mere kaunse piece
    par khatra hai?
2.  Target: kaunse pieces bina protection hain? King kitna khula hai? Kaun do
    kaam kar raha hai?
3.  Candidates: 2-3 chaalein chuno. Pehle checks, phir captures, phir threats.
4.  Line: ek candidate ko end tak socho. Har chaal par opponent ka sabse
    forcing jawab lo.
5.  Ruko: line tab roko jab koi check, capture ya threat na bache. Phir
    material gino.
6.  Blunder check: chalne se pehle dekho ki opponent ke paas koi check,
    capture ya threat to nahi bacha.

    Kya chhodna hai (skip rules)
    ============================

1.  Pehle forcing chaalein dekho. Quiet chaal tabhi socho jab forcing se kuch
    na mile.
2.  Jo chaal bina check ya mate ke apna piece latka de, use chhod do.
3.  Opponent ke jawab mein pehle uske checks aur captures dekho, phir 1 best
    defence. Baaki jawab chhod do.
4.  Har line ek hi baar socho aur end mein uska nateeja yaad rakho (+3,
    barabar, mate). Dobara mat socho.
5.  Ek candidate saaf jeet de raha ho to baaki chhod do. Simple jeet sundar
    jeet se behtar hai.
6.  Jahan position shaant ho jaaye, wahin ruko. Usse aage sochna time ki
    barbaadi hai.

    Ladder: depth, steps, target time, rules lesson
    ===============================================

  Depth   Steps                                                Target   Pehle yeh lesson
  ------- ---------------------------------------------------- -------- --------------------------------------------------
  1       Asaan · Medium · Andhe · Mushkil · GM                15s      4.6 Calculation 1: sochne ka routine aur 1 chaal
  2       Asaan · Medium · Andhe · Mushkil · Poori line · GM   30s      6.5 Calculation 2: skip rules aur 2 chaal
  3       Asaan · Medium · Andhe · Mushkil · Poori line · GM   50s      6.6 Calculation 3: 3-5 chaal ka tree
  4       Asaan · Medium · Andhe · Mushkil · Poori line · GM   75s      6.6 Calculation 3: 3-5 chaal ka tree
  5       Asaan · Medium · Andhe · Mushkil · Poori line · GM   100s     6.6 Calculation 3: 3-5 chaal ka tree
  6       Asaan · Medium · Andhe · Mushkil · Poori line · GM   140s     14.6 Calculation 4: Legendary 6-8 chaal
  7       Asaan · Medium · Andhe · Mushkil · Poori line · GM   180s     14.6 Calculation 4: Legendary 6-8 chaal
  8       Asaan · Medium · Andhe · Mushkil · Poori line · GM   240s     14.6 Calculation 4: Legendary 6-8 chaal

Target time "board dekh ke" steps ka hai. Andhe, Poori line aur GM steps mein
1.5 guna time milta hai.

    Kis level ke saath kaunse steps
    ===============================

  Level test                                                  Gym steps   Kahan tak pass
  ----------------------------------------------------------- ----------- ------------------

    ######################################################################
    ##  LEVEL 5: MIDDLEGAME 1: TACTICS KE HATHIYAAR
    ######################################################################

Sochne ki process, fork, pin, skewer, discovered attack, defender hatao

-   Middlegame section yahan se shuru (Level 5-9).
-   Middlegame woh hissa hai jab pieces bahar aa chuke hain aur asli ladaai
    hoti hai.
-   Pehle sochne ki process, phir ek-ek tactic, har ek ke saath bahut saare
    puzzles.

    5.1 Middlegame mein sochne ki process
    =====================================

Processes vs patterns, downsides, defensive hero, ek-chaal ka game

Yaad rakho

-   Process (calculation, evaluation, visualization) aur pattern (fork, pin,
    mate patterns) alag cheezein hain. Dono chahiye.
-   Chess "single-turn" game hai: ek chaal mein do threats = opponent ek hi
    bacha payega (double attack ka raaz).
-   Evaluation mein opponent ke downsides dhundo: unprotected pieces, kamzor
    king.
-   Calculation ek "draft story" hai: jo defensive hero tumhari story bigaadta
    hai, use hatao.
-   Forcing moves dekho, par kabhi quiet chaal aur kabhi aur aage calculate
    karna behtar.

Is lesson ke topics

-   5.1.1 Processes aur patterns
-   5.1.2 Chess ek-chaal ka game hai
-   5.1.3 Sawal 1: Opponent ki do cheezon par ek saath threat hai, aur koi ek
    chaal…
-   5.1.4 Downsides ka jashn: unprotected pieces aur king
-   5.1.5 Defensive hero ko hatao
-   5.1.6 Sawal 2: Tumhari mate ki line mein ek black knight f6 square h7 ko
    bacha…
-   5.1.7 Facts awareness
-   5.1.8 Abhyaas: Process lagao
-   5.1.9 Abhyaas: Process lagao

    5.1.1 Processes aur patterns
    ----------------------------

-   Process = sochne ka tareeka, kisi bhi position mein: forcing moves
    (checks, captures, threats) dekhna, calculation (aage ki chaalein sochna),
    visualization (dimaag mein board), evaluation (line ke end mein kaun
    behtar: material, king safety, pawn structure).
-   Pattern = jaani-pehchaani shakal: fork, pin, deflection, mate patterns.
-   Pattern pata na ho tab bhi process se jeet sakte ho.
-   Aur patterns process ko tez karte hain.

    5.1.2 Chess ek-chaal ka game hai
    --------------------------------

-   Har chaal mein opponent sirf ek kaam kar sakta hai.
-   Agar tum ek chaal se do threats banao, aur koi ek chaal dono ko na roke,
    to ek cheez jaayegi.
-   Isliye double attack (fork) sabse important tactic hai.
-   Kabhi double attack ek piece nahi karta: ek piece hatne se peeche wale ka
    attack khulta hai (discovered attack).

    5.1.3 Sawal 1: Opponent ki do cheezon par ek saath threat hai, aur koi ek chaal…
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Opponent ki do cheezon par ek saath threat hai, aur koi ek chaal dono nahi
bachati. Kyun jeet hoti hai?

Jawab:

✓ Kyunki chess mein ek turn mein ek hi chaal hoti hai

-   Single-turn game: ek chaal = ek bachaav.

    5.1.4 Downsides ka jashn: unprotected pieces aur king
    -----------------------------------------------------

-   Position dekh kar pehle opponent ki kamzoriyan (downsides) dhundo:
    -   Unprotected pieces: sabse classic downside. Kabhi seedha nahi pakad
        sakte, use doosre downside (jaise check) mein badalna padta hai.
    -   King safety: khula king, kamzor back rank, pawns aage badhe hue.
-   Calculation in downsides ko ubhaarne ka tool hai.
-   Pata ho ki kya dhundh rahe ho, to aur aage tak dekh paate ho.

    5.1.5 Defensive hero ko hatao
    -----------------------------

-   Calculation ek draft story jaisi hai.
-   Aksar story mein ek defensive hero hota hai: woh ek piece jo tumhara plan
    bigaad deta hai (mate square bachata hai, piece ko protect karta hai).
-   Story dobara likho: pehle hero ko capture karo, bhagao, ya bhatkao
    (removing the defender, deflection), phir plan chalao.

    5.1.6 Sawal 2: Tumhari mate ki line mein ek black knight f6 square h7 ko bacha…
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Tumhari mate ki line mein ek black knight f6 square h7 ko bacha raha hai.
Pehle kya socho?

Jawab:

✓ Knight (defensive hero) ko kaise hatayein

-   Defender hatao, phir mate.

    5.1.7 Facts awareness
    ---------------------

-   Har forcing chaal khelni zaroori nahi: kabhi quiet chaal (jo badi threat
    banaye) zyada strong.
-   Opponent ki threat aur trap se jaagruk raho, par darr ke ruk mat jao:
    kabhi aur aage calculate karo to dikhega ki "khatra" asli nahi.

    5.1.8 Abhyaas: Process lagao
    ----------------------------

    5.2 Fork (do pe ek saath attack)
    ================================

Knight, queen aur pawn forks

Yaad rakho

-   Fork = ek piece se do ya zyada pieces par ek saath attack.
-   Knight fork sabse khatarnaak: uska attack block nahi hota.
-   Best fork = check + kuch aur: pehle king bachana padta hai.
-   Pawn aur queen bhi fork karte hain. Royal fork = king + queen.
-   Fork = double attack. King + kuch aur par fork sabse strong (check ka
    jawab dena padta hai).
-   Pawn bhi fork karta hai. Fork se pawn ko promote karne ka raasta bhi ban
    sakta hai.
-   Famous: Petrosian-Spassky, Petrosian-Simagin, Botvinnik-Kotov,
    Anand-Kasparov, Tal-Pliss (pawn fork).

Is lesson ke topics

-   5.2.1 Fork kya hai
-   5.2.2 Abhyaas: Knight fork dhundo!
-   5.2.3 Abhyaas: Queen fork
-   5.2.4 Abhyaas: Pawn bhi fork karta hai!
-   5.2.5 Abhyaas: Royal fork
-   5.2.6 Masters ke forks
-   5.2.7 Abhyaas: Fork dhundo
-   5.2.8 Abhyaas: Fork dhundo
-   5.2.9 Abhyaas: Fork dhundo
-   5.2.10 Abhyaas: Fork dhundo
-   5.2.11 Abhyaas: Fork dhundo
-   5.2.12 Abhyaas: Fork dhundo
-   5.2.13 Abhyaas: Fork dhundo
-   5.2.14 Abhyaas: Fork dhundo

    5.2.1 Fork kya hai
    ------------------

-   Fork = ek piece se do ya zyada dushman pieces par ek saath attack.
-   Opponent ek hi bacha sakta hai.
-   Knight fork sabse khatarnaak hota hai kyunki knight ka attack block nahi
    hota.
-   Sabse best fork: king + kuch aur (check ke saath), kyunki check pehle
    bachana padta hai.

    8 | r . . . k . . .
    7 | p p . . . p p p
    6 | . . N . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | P P . . . P P P
    1 | . . . . K . . .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    5.2.2 Abhyaas: Knight fork dhundo!
    ----------------------------------

Knight fork dhundo!

    5.2.4 Abhyaas: Pawn bhi fork karta hai!
    ---------------------------------------

Pawn bhi fork karta hai!

    8 | . . . . k . . .
    7 | p p . . . . p p

    5.2.6 Masters ke forks
    ----------------------

-   Fork har level par aata hai:
    -   Petrosian vs Spassky, Petrosian vs Simagin, Botvinnik vs Kotov: knight
        aur queen ke forks se material.
    -   Avrukh vs Kamsky: fork se pawn ko queen banane ka raasta saaf.
    -   Aronian vs Seel, Anand vs Kasparov, Keene vs Botvinnik.
    -   Tal vs Pliss: "pawns bhi fork karte hain!"
-   Ab khud karo: aur forks.

Line ke baad position

    5.3 Pin
    =======

Piece hil nahi sakta

Yaad rakho

-   Pin: aage wala piece hil nahi sakta kyunki peeche zyada keemti piece hai.
-   Absolute pin (peeche king): kanoonan hil nahi sakta. Relative pin (peeche
    queen/rook): hilega to nuksaan.
-   Pinned piece par aur attack karo (aksar pawn se).
-   Bishop, rook, queen hi pin karte hain.
-   Absolute pin: peeche king, pinned piece chal hi nahi sakta. Relative pin:
    peeche queen/rook, chal sakta hai par nuksaan.
-   Relative pin kabhi tod sakte ho (rebel) agar badle mein bada fayda ho
    (Legal trap, Popov-Petkovski).
-   Pinned piece defender nahi raha: pin se mate ya material jeeto
    (Nimzowitsch-Rubinstein).

Is lesson ke topics

-   5.3.1 Pin kya hai
-   5.3.2 Abhyaas: Pin karke queen jeeto
-   5.3.3 Abhyaas: Pinned knight par attack karo!
-   5.3.4 Sawal 1: Yahan c6 ka knight kis type ke pin mein hai?
-   5.3.5 Pin ke prakaar
-   5.3.6 Elephant trap (Queen's Gambit Declined): pin jo pin nahi tha
-   5.3.7 Sawal 2: Pinned piece ke peeche king hai
-   5.3.8 Abhyaas: Pin se jeeto
-   5.3.9 Abhyaas: Pin se jeeto
-   5.3.10 Abhyaas: Pin se jeeto
-   5.3.11 Abhyaas: Pin se jeeto
-   5.3.12 Abhyaas: Pin se jeeto
-   5.3.13 Abhyaas: Pin se jeeto

    5.3.1 Pin kya hai
    -----------------

-   Pin = ek piece hil nahi sakta (ya hilna nahi chahiye) kyunki uske peeche
    zyada keemti piece hai.
-   Absolute pin: peeche king hai. Pinned piece kanoonan hil hi nahi sakta.
-   Relative pin: peeche queen/rook hai. Hil sakta hai par nuksaan hoga.
-   Pin ka fayda: pinned piece par aur attack karo (jaise pawn se).

    8 | r . b q k . . r
    7 | p p p p . p p p
    6 | . . n . . n . .
    5 | . B . . p . . .
    4 | . . . . P . . .
    3 | . . . . . N . .
    2 | P P P P . P P P
    1 | R N B Q K . . R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

Jawab:

✓ Absolute pin (peeche king)

-   Bb5 se e8 king tak diagonal: knight beech mein hai aur kanoonan hil nahi
    sakta.

    5.3.5 Pin ke prakaar
    --------------------

-   Absolute pin: peeche king. Pinned piece chal hi nahi sakta (illegal).
-   Relative pin: peeche queen ya rook. Chal sakta hai, par peeche wala
    jaayega.
-   Relative pin se bagawat: kabhi pinned piece chala do agar badle mein mate
    ya bada fayda ho (Legal trap mein Nxe5!, Popov vs Petkovski).
-   Pin ka jashn: pinned piece "nakli defender" hai. Us par aur attackers lao
    (Nimzowitsch vs Rubinstein: pin se mate force).
-   Examples: Nimzowitsch-Nielsen (defensive hero ko pin karke hataaya),
    Bronstein-Goldenov (pin se key square par kabza), Xie Jun-Yu Lefu.

    5.3.6 Elephant trap (Queen's Gambit Declined): pin jo pin nahi tha
    ------------------------------------------------------------------

-   White ko laga f6 knight pinned hai aur d5 pawn free hai.
-   Par Black ka jawab tayyar tha.

1. d4 d5 2. c4 e6 3. Nc3 Nf6 4. Bg5 Nbd7 5. cxd5 exd5 6. Nxd5 Nxd5 7. Bxd8
Bb4+ 8. Qd2 Bxd2+ 9. Kxd2 Kxd8

-   4...Nbd7 trap set.
-   6.Nxd5?? "knight pinned hai" soch kar pawn liya.
-   6...Nxd5! relative pin todi! Queen de di.
-   7.Bxd8
-   7...Bb4+! check.
-   8.Qd2 sirf yahi.
-   8...Bxd2+
-   9...Kxd8 Black ek piece aage.

    1 | R N B K Q . . R
    2 | P P P P . . P P
    3 | . . . . . N . .
    4 | . . . . P P . .
    5 | . B . . p . . .
    6 | . . n p . . . .
    7 | p p p . n p p p
    8 | r . b k q b . r

    5.4 Skewer
    ==========

Ulta pin

Yaad rakho

-   Skewer = ulta pin: keemti piece aage (aksar king), kam keemti peeche.
-   Aage wala hatega, peeche wala capture.
-   Line pieces (rook, bishop, queen) hi skewer karte hain.
-   Skewer = ulta pin: aage bada piece (king/queen), hatega to peeche wala
    jaayega.
-   Famous: Short-Vaganian, Kasparov-Tatai, Fischer-Stepans, Carlsen-Anand,
    Kramnik-Nakamura.

Is lesson ke topics

-   5.4.1 Skewer
-   5.4.2 Abhyaas: Rook skewer!
-   5.4.3 Abhyaas: Bishop skewer!
-   5.4.4 Masters ke skewers
-   5.4.5 Abhyaas: Skewer dhundo
-   5.4.6 Abhyaas: Skewer dhundo
-   5.4.7 Abhyaas: Skewer dhundo
-   5.4.8 Abhyaas: Skewer dhundo
-   5.4.9 Abhyaas: Skewer dhundo
-   5.4.10 Abhyaas: Skewer dhundo

    5.4.1 Skewer
    ------------

-   Skewer pin ka ulta hai: keemti piece aage hai (aksar king), aur kam keemti
    peeche.
-   Aage wala hatega, to peeche wala capture.
-   Rooks, bishops aur queens skewer karte hain (seedhi line wale pieces).

    8 | . . . . . . . .
    7 | . . . . . . . .
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | R . k . . . q .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . . . K
    1 | . . . . . . . .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

Line ke baad position

    5.4.4 Masters ke skewers
    ------------------------

-   Skewer mein bada piece aage hota hai.
-   Woh hatta hai, peeche wala jaata hai.
-   Aksar check ke saath.
-   Examples: Short-Vaganian, Kasparov-Tatai, Fischer-Stepans, Unzicker-Tal,
    Carlsen-Anand, Spassky-Korchnoi, Kramnik-Nakamura, Rivera-Fischer.

    5.4.5 Abhyaas: Skewer dhundo
    ----------------------------

Skewer dhundo: bade piece par attack, peeche wala jeeto.

    1 | R . . K . . . .
    2 | . P . . . . . .
    3 | . . . P . . R .
    4 | . . N . . r p .
    5 | p P . . . . . .
    6 | . p . . . n . .
    7 | . k . . . . . .
    8 | r . . . . . . .

        h g f e d c b a

Line ke baad position

    5.5 Discovered attack aur double check
    ======================================

Chhupa hua attack

Yaad rakho

-   Discovered attack: aage wala piece hate to peeche wale ka attack khul
    jaata hai.
-   Hatne wala piece bhi kuch attack kare = do threats ek saath.
-   Double check ka jawab sirf king ko hatana hai: block ya capture nahi.
-   Discovered attack: aage wala piece hat-ta hai aur khud bhi kuch attack
    karta hai = do threats.
-   Discovered check sabse khatarnak: aage wala piece kuch bhi le sakta hai.
-   Famous: Reti-Tartakower, Bronstein-Geller, Fischer-Spassky 1972 (game 8),
    Kasparov-Karpov.

Is lesson ke topics

-   5.5.1 Discovered attack
-   5.5.2 Abhyaas: Discovered check se queen jeeto
-   5.5.3 Abhyaas: Double check se mate in 1!
-   5.5.4 Masters ke discovered attacks
-   5.5.5 Abhyaas: Discovered attack
-   5.5.6 Abhyaas: Discovered attack
-   5.5.7 Abhyaas: Discovered attack

-   5.5.9 Abhyaas: Discovered attack
-   5.5.10 Abhyaas: Discovered attack
-   5.5.11 Abhyaas: Discovered check se jeeto!
-   5.5.12 Abhyaas: Discovered check se jeeto!
-   5.5.13 Abhyaas: Discovered check se jeeto!

    5.5.1 Discovered attack
    -----------------------

-   Jab ek piece hatta hai aur uske peeche wale piece ka attack khul jaata
    hai, use discovered attack kehte hain.
-   Agar khula attack king par ho to discovered check.
-   Khatarnaak kyunki hatne wala piece bhi kuch attack kar sakta hai: do
    threats ek saath!
-   Double check: dono pieces check dein.
-   Iska jawab sirf king ko hatana hai.

    8 | . . . . k . . .
    7 | . . . . . . . .
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . N . . .
    3 | . . q . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . . . .
    1 | . . . . R . K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

Chaal ke baad

    5.5.4 Masters ke discovered attacks
    -----------------------------------

-   Examples: Reti-Tartakower (queen sacrifice ke baad discovered double
    check), Bronstein-Geller, Fischer-Spassky 1972 (game 8), Kasparov-Karpov,
    Reti-Bogoljubov, Petrosian-Matanovic.
-   Yaad rakho: jo piece hat-ta hai, use bhi kaam do (capture, check ya
    threat).

Line ke baad position

    5.6 Defender hatao aur overloading
    ==================================

Removing the defender (undermining), overloaded piece

Yaad rakho

-   Removing the defender: bachaane wale piece ko capture karo ya bhagao.
-   Deflection: defender ko zabardasti door le jao.
-   Overloading: ek defender ke do kaam, ek par vaar karo.
-   Decoy: dushman piece ko kharab square par lalchao.
-   Removing the defender (undermining / removing the guard): defender ko
    capture karo ya bhagao.
-   Overloading: ek piece ke paas do kaam; ek kaam par hamla karo, doosra
    chhoot jaayega.
-   Examples: Tseshkovsky-Kasparov, Karjakin-Short, Adams-Whiteley,
    Uhlmann-Schwartz.

Is lesson ke topics

-   5.6.1 Defender ko hatao
-   5.6.2 Abhyaas: Defender hatao, phir mate ki threat
-   5.6.3 Abhyaas: Deflection
-   5.6.4 Undermining ke examples
-   5.6.5 Abhyaas: Pehle defender ko hatao, phir jeeto
-   5.6.6 Abhyaas: Pehle defender ko hatao, phir jeeto
-   5.6.7 Abhyaas: Pehle defender ko hatao, phir jeeto
-   5.6.8 Abhyaas: Pehle defender ko hatao, phir jeeto
-   5.6.9 Abhyaas: Pehle defender ko hatao, phir jeeto
-   5.6.10 Abhyaas: Pehle defender ko hatao, phir jeeto

    5.6.1 Defender ko hatao
    -----------------------

-   Removing the defender: jo piece kisi square/piece ko bacha raha hai, use
    capture karo ya bhagao.
-   Deflection: defender ko zabardasti door le jao (aksar sacrifice se).
-   Overloading: ek defender ke paas do kaam hain. Ek kaam par attack karo,
    doosra khul jaayega.
-   Decoy: dushman piece ko kharab square par lalchao (sacrifice se).

    5.6.2 Abhyaas: Defender hatao, phir mate ki threat
    --------------------------------------------------

Defender hatao, phir mate ki threat.

    8 | r . . . . r k .
    7 | p p . . . p p p
    6 | . . . . . n . .
    5 | . . . . . . B Q
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . B . . . .
    2 | P P . . . P P P
    1 | R . . . . . K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

(White ki chaal)

Jawab:

1. Bxf6

-   Nf6 hi h7 ko bacha raha tha.
-   Bxf6 ke baad Qxh7# ki threat, isliye White piece jeet leta hai.

    8 | r . . . . r k .
    7 | p p . . . p p p
    6 | . . . . . B . .
    5 | . . . . . . . Q
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . B . . . .
    2 | P P . . . P P P
    1 | R . . . . . K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    5.6.3 Abhyaas: Deflection
    -------------------------

Deflection: queen le lo! Socho: kya Black wapas le sakta hai?

    8 | . . . . r . k .
    7 | . . . . q p p p
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . Q . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . P P P
    1 | . . . R . . K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

(White ki chaal)

Jawab:

1. Qxe7

-   Qxe7! Agar ...Rxe7 to Rd8+ Re8 Rxe8#: rook ko back rank chhodni padi.
-   Isliye Black wapas nahi le sakta aur queen gayi.

    8 | . . . . r . k .
    7 | . . . . Q p p p
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .

        a b c d e f g h

Chaal ke baad

    5.6.4 Undermining ke examples
    -----------------------------

-   Removing the defender ko undermining ya removing the guard bhi kehte hain.
-   Examples: Tseshkovsky-Kasparov, Karjakin-Short, Uhlmann-Schwartz,
    Adams-Whiteley, Inkiov-Jovanic, Cukierman vs NN.

    ######################################################################
    ##  LEVEL 6: MIDDLEGAME 2: ADVANCED TACTICS AUR CALCULATION
    ######################################################################

Deflection, decoy, interference, clearance, x-ray, zwischenzug, golden rules,
calculation

-   Ab bade hathiyaar: deflection, decoy, interference, clearance, x-ray aur
    zwischenzug.
-   Phir calculation ke golden rules aur visualization.

    6.1 Deflection aur decoy
    ========================

Defender ko door bhagao, ya dushman piece ko kharab square par kheencho

Yaad rakho

-   Deflection: defender ko uski duty se door kheencho (aksar sacrifice se).
-   Decoy: dushman piece (aksar king/queen) ko kisi kharab square par laao,
    phir fork/skewer/mate.
-   Famous deflection: Timman-Yusupov, Zukertort-Blackburne, Velimirovic-Csom,
    Botvinnik-Keres, Tal-Averbakh.
-   Famous decoy: Bjornsson-Tal, Nimzowitsch-Mieses, Seirawan-Polgar,
    Letelier-Fischer, Korchnoi-Tal, Polgar-Hansen.

Is lesson ke topics

-   6.1.1 Deflection
-   6.1.2 Abhyaas: Deflection

-   6.1.4 Abhyaas: Deflection

    kheencho

    kheencho

    6.1.1 Deflection
    ----------------

-   Deflection = defender ko uske kaam se door le jao.
-   Jaise ek rook back rank bacha raha hai: use kisi aur cheez par capture
    karne pe majboor karo, phir back rank par mate.
-   Examples: Timman-Yusupov, Chandler-Gruenfeld, Zukertort-Blackburne,
    Velimirovic-Csom, Canal vs NN, Botvinnik-Keres, Chiburdanidze-Malaniuk,
    Tal-Averbakh, Lputian-Balashov.

    6.1.2 Abhyaas: Deflection
    -------------------------

Deflection: defender ko uski duty se hatao.

    8 | . . . r r . . .
    7 | . . . . q p k .
    6 | . p . p p . . p
    5 | p N p . n P N .
    4 | P . . . b . P .
    3 | . P . P . . P .
    2 | . . P Q R . B K
    1 | . . . . . R . .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

(White ki chaal)

Jawab:

1. f6+ Qxf6 2. Rxf6

-   Line: f6+ Qxf6 Rxf6.
-   Defender capture karne gaya aur apna asli kaam chhod diya.

    8 | . . . r r . . .
    7 | . . . . . p k .
    6 | . p . p p R . p
    5 | p N p . n . N .
    4 | P . . . b . P .
    3 | . P . P . . P .
    2 | . . P Q R . B K
    1 | . . . . . . . .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    6.1.8 Decoy (attraction)
    ------------------------

-   Decoy = sacrifice se dushman ke piece (aksar king ya queen) ko ek kharab
    square par kheencho.
-   Wahan fork, skewer, pin ya mate ho jaata hai.
-   Examples: Bjornsson-Tal, Nimzowitsch-Mieses, Seirawan-Polgar,
    Letelier-Fischer, Nei-Petrosian, Spassky-Averkin, Korchnoi-Tal,
    Larsen-Spassky, Kosten-Chandler, Kotronias-Vallejo, Polgar-Hansen.

    6.1.9 Sawal 1: Deflection aur decoy mein farak?
    -----------------------------------------------

Deflection aur decoy mein farak?

Jawab:

✓ Deflection defender ko kisi jagah se hatata hai, decoy dushman piece ko kisi
jagah par laata hai

-   Deflection = "wahan se hatao", decoy = "yahan laao".

    6.1.10 Abhyaas: Decoy: sacrifice se dushman piece ko kharab square par kheencho
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Decoy: sacrifice se dushman piece ko kharab square par kheencho.

    8 | r n b q k b . r
    7 | p p p . . p . p
    6 | . . . . p . . .
    5 | . . . p P . p .
    4 | . . . P N . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | P P P . . P P P
    1 | R . B Q K B N R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    hai.

    7 | . b k . . p . .
    8 | . . . . . . . .

        h g f e d c b a

    6.2 Interference, clearance, x-ray, zwischenzug
    ===============================================

Lines kaatna aur kholna, aar-paar attack, beech ki chaal

Yaad rakho

-   Zwischenzug: recapture se pehle ek zyada forcing chaal.
-   X-ray: piece dushman piece ke aar-paar square control karta hai.
-   Interference: do dushman pieces ke beech apna piece rakh kar connection
    kaato.
-   Clearance, desperado, perpetual check: ye bhi yaad rakho.
-   Interference (disconnection, "spanner"): do dushman pieces ke beech apna
    piece rakh kar unka connection kaato.
-   Clearance (line clearance): apna hi piece raaste se hatao (aksar tempo ke
    saath) taaki doosre piece ki line khule.
-   X-ray: piece doosre piece ke aar-paar square ko control/support karta hai.
-   Zwischenzug: expected recapture se pehle ek zyada forcing chaal.

Is lesson ke topics

-   6.2.1 Advanced motifs
-   6.2.2 Sawal 1: Opponent ne tumhara knight capture kiya
-   6.2.3 Sawal 2: Tum haar rahe ho, par opponent ke king ko lagaataar check
    de sakte ho
-   6.2.4 Interference (spanner in the works)
-   6.2.5 Sawal 3: Black ka rook b8 aur queen g8 ek doosre ko back rank par
    bachate hain
-   6.2.6 Clearance (line kholna)
-   6.2.7 Sawal 4: Tumhari queen ki h-file par tumhara apna knight h5 baitha
    hai
-   6.2.8 X-ray
-   6.2.9 Abhyaas: X-ray: queens aamne-saamne, dono ke peeche rooks
-   6.2.10 Abhyaas: Zwischenzug!
-   6.2.11 Diagram: X-ray: Rd1 ka attack apne Rd2 ke aar-paar d8 tak
    (doubling)

    6.2.1 Advanced motifs
    ---------------------

-   Zwischenzug (in-between move): expected recapture ki jagah pehle ek zyada
    forcing chaal (check/threat) khelo.
-   X-ray: piece dushman piece ke aar-paar ek square ko control karta hai.
-   Interference: do dushman pieces ke beech mein apna piece rakh kar unka
    connection kaato.
-   Clearance: apne hi piece ko hata kar line/square khaali karo.
-   Desperado: jo piece waise bhi jaane wala hai, use jitna ho sake mehenga
    becho.
-   Perpetual check: haarti position mein lagaataar check de kar draw.

    6.2.2 Sawal 1: Opponent ne tumhara knight capture kiya
    ------------------------------------------------------

Opponent ne tumhara knight capture kiya. Recapture ki jagah tum pehle check
dete ho jo queen ko bhi attack karta hai. Yeh kya kehlata hai?

Jawab:

✓ Zwischenzug

-   In-between move: pehle bada threat, recapture baad mein.

    6.2.3 Sawal 2: Tum haar rahe ho, par opponent ke king ko lagaataar check de sakte ho
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Tum haar rahe ho, par opponent ke king ko lagaataar check de sakte ho. Kya
milega?

Jawab:

✓ Draw (perpetual check / repetition)

-   Lagaataar checks se same position repeat hoti hai = draw.

    6.2.4 Interference (spanner in the works)
    -----------------------------------------

-   Do dushman pieces ek doosre ko support kar rahe hain (jaise do rooks, ya
    rook jo mate square bachata hai).
-   Unke beech mein apna piece rakho: ab line kat gayi.
-   Use capture karne mein bhi line aksar band rehti hai.
-   Examples: Miles-Pritchett, Piket-Kramnik, Kramnik-Topalov,
    Koskinen-Kasman, Karpov-Polgar.

    6.2.5 Sawal 3: Black ka rook b8 aur queen g8 ek doosre ko back rank par bachate hain
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Black ka rook b8 aur queen g8 ek doosre ko back rank par bachate hain. Tum e8
par piece rakh do jo dono line kaat de. Yeh kaunsa tactic?

Jawab:

✓ Interference

-   Line ke beech piece rakhna = interference.

    6.2.6 Clearance (line kholna)
    -----------------------------

-   Kabhi apna hi piece raasta rok raha hota hai.
-   Use tempo ke saath (check, capture ya threat se) hatao taaki
    queen/rook/bishop ki line khul jaaye, ya square khaali ho jaaye.
-   Examples: Keene-Briant, Karjakin-Komiliakov, La Bourdonnais-McDonnell.

    6.2.7 Sawal 4: Tumhari queen ki h-file par tumhara apna knight h5 baitha hai
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Tumhari queen ki h-file par tumhara apna knight h5 baitha hai. Knight check
dekar hatta hai aur queen h7 par mate karti hai. Yeh kya hai?

Jawab:

✓ Clearance

-   Apna piece hata kar line kholna = clearance.

    6.2.8 X-ray
    -----------

-   Piece doosre piece ke aar-paar dekhta hai.
-   Jaise rook queen ke peeche ho (battery): queen trade hone par rook usi
    square ko support karta hai.
-   Examples: Fischer-Bisguier, Euwe-Loman, Ivanchuk-Van Wely,
    Mamedyarov-Carlsen.

    6.2.9 Abhyaas: X-ray: queens aamne-saamne, dono ke peeche rooks
    ---------------------------------------------------------------

X-ray: queens aamne-saamne, dono ke peeche rooks. Kaun jeetega? Chaal chalo.

-   White ek rook aage.

X-ray: Rd1 ka attack apne Rd2 ke aar-paar d8 tak (doubling)

    6.3 Calculation ke 3 golden rules
    =================================

Pichli chaal ki kamzori, killer common squares, in-effect clause, squares ka
pyaar

Yaad rakho

-   Rule 1: Opponent ki pichli chaal ne kya kamzor chhoda? (square, piece,
    line)
-   Rule 2: Killer common squares: jahan tumhare do pieces mil kar attack
    karte hain, wahan sacrifice ya mate aksar kaam karta hai.
-   Rule 3: In-effect clause: har chaal ka poore board par asar dekho
    (discovered lines, khule raaste, open/closed position).
-   Teeno ka raaz ek: chess squares ka khel hai. Jeetne ke liye key squares
    tak pahuncho.

Is lesson ke topics

-   6.3.1 Calculation kya hai?
-   6.3.2 Rule 1: Pichli chaal ki kamzori
-   6.3.3 Rule 2: Killer common squares
-   6.3.4 Abhyaas: Common square dhundo jahan tumhare pieces mil kar mate
    karein
-   6.3.5 Abhyaas: Common square dhundo jahan tumhare pieces mil kar mate
    karein
-   6.3.6 Abhyaas: Common square dhundo jahan tumhare pieces mil kar mate
    karein
-   6.3.7 Abhyaas: Common square dhundo jahan tumhare pieces mil kar mate
    karein
-   6.3.8 Rule 3: In-effect clause
-   6.3.9 Abhyaas: In-effect clause
-   6.3.10 Abhyaas: In-effect clause
-   6.3.11 Abhyaas: In-effect clause
-   6.3.12 Squares ka pyaar
-   6.3.13 Sawal 1: Teeno golden rules mein common cheez kya hai?

    6.3.1 Calculation kya hai?
    --------------------------

-   Calculation = aage ki chaalein dimaag mein khelna: meri chaal, opponent ka
    sabse achha jawab, phir meri... aur end mein evaluate karna.
-   Forcing moves se shuru karo kyunki unke jawab kam hote hain.
-   Is course ke teen golden rules calculation ko asaan banate hain.

    6.3.2 Rule 1: Pichli chaal ki kamzori
    -------------------------------------

-   Har chaal kuch naya kholti hai aur kuch chhodti hai.
-   Opponent ke chalne ke baad pucho:
    -   Is piece ne kaunsa square/piece bachana chhod diya?
    -   Kaunsi line khul gayi?
    -   Kya koi piece ab unprotected hai?

    6.3.3 Rule 2: Killer common squares
    -----------------------------------

-   Woh squares jahan tumhare do (ya zyada) pieces ek saath pahunchte hain.
-   Wahan:
    -   sacrifice karke bhi recapture ho jaata hai,
    -   ek piece doosre ko support karke mate deta hai (jaise queen + bishop
        h7 par).
-   Kabhi common square shuru se hota hai, kabhi calculation ke beech banta
    hai.
-   Dovetail aur Mayet jaise mate patterns isi par chalte hain.

    6.3.4 Abhyaas: Common square dhundo jahan tumhare pieces mil kar mate karein
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Common square dhundo jahan tumhare pieces mil kar mate karein.

    8 | . r k . . b r .
    7 | p . . . . B . p
    6 | . p n . . . . .
    5 | . N . . . . . P
    4 | P . . . p . . .
    3 | . . Q . . . . .
    2 | . P . . K P q .
    1 | R . . . . . . R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

(White ki chaal)

Jawab:

1. Qxc6+ Kd8 2. Qc7#

-   Line: Qxc6+ Kd8 Qc7#.
-   Do pieces ek hi square/area par mile: wahi killer common square.

    8 | . r . k . b r .
    7 | p . Q . . B . p
    6 | . p . . . . . .
    5 | . N . . . . . P
    4 | P . . . p . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . P . . K P q .
    1 | R . . . . . . R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    6.3.8 Rule 3: In-effect clause
    ------------------------------

-   Sirf jo piece chala uske arrows mat dekho.
-   Poore board par asar dekho:
    -   Hatne se kaunsi line khuli (discovered effects)?
    -   Kaunse doosre pieces azaad hue?
    -   Pawns ka role: open position (khule raaste) mein tactics zyada, closed
        mein kam.

    6.3.9 Abhyaas: In-effect clause
    -------------------------------

In-effect clause: chaal ka poore board par asar dekho.

    8 | r n b q . r k .
    7 | p p p . . p p .
    6 | . . . . p n . p
    5 | . N b . N . . .
    4 | . . P P . . . .
    3 | . . . . . B . .
    2 | P P . . . P P P
    1 | . R B Q . R K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

(White ki chaal)

Jawab:

-   Ek chaal ne doosre piece ki line kholi.

    8 | r n b q . r k .

    6 | . . . . p n . p
    5 | . N P . N . . .
    4 | . . P . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . B . .

        a b c d e f g h

    6.3.12 Squares ka pyaar
    -----------------------

-   Teeno rules ek hi baat kehte hain: chess squares ka khel hai.
-   Pichli chaal ki kamzori = neglected squares, common squares = cooperation
    wale squares, in-effect = naye khule squares.
-   Spassky ne Fischer ke against 1972 mein ek ajeeb chaal se key squares
    jeete.

    6.3.13 Sawal 1: Teeno golden rules mein common cheez kya hai?
    -------------------------------------------------------------

Teeno golden rules mein common cheez kya hai?

Jawab:

✓ Squares par kabza

-   Chess is about squares.

    6.4 Calculation aur visualization
    =================================

Dimaag mein board dekhna

Yaad rakho

-   2-4 candidate moves chuno (pehle CCT).
-   Har line ek-ek karke dekho, opponent ka sabse achha jawab socho.
-   Line ke end mein position evaluate karo.
-   Visualization: notation padh kar board dimaag mein dekho, blindfold
    puzzles karo.
-   Visualization: board ke bina pieces ki jagah dimaag mein rakho (The
    Queen's Gambit mein Beth ceiling par board dekhti thi).

Is lesson ke topics

-   6.4.1 Calculation kaise karein
-   6.4.2 Sawal 1: Board nahi dikhega
-   6.4.3 Sawal 2: Dimaag mein
-   6.4.4 Sawal 3: Bina board
-   6.4.5 Sawal 4: Bina board
-   6.4.6 Sawal 5: Dimaag mein

    6.4.1 Calculation kaise karein
    ------------------------------

-   Candidate moves chuno: 2-4 achhi chaalein (pehle CCT).
-   Har ek ko ek-ek karke dekho, beech mein mat koodo.
-   Opponent ka sabse achha jawab socho, sabse bura nahi.
-   Line ke end mein position ko evaluate karo: kaun behtar?
-   Forcing lines (check, capture) pehle aur gehri dekho: inme opponent ke
    option kam hote hain.
-   Visualization badhane ke liye: blindfold puzzles, notation padh kar board
    dimaag mein dekhna.
-   Iske liye Mind's Eye trainer (neeche Trainer tab) bana hi hai!

    6.4.2 Sawal 1: Board nahi dikhega
    ---------------------------------

Board nahi dikhega. Socho: White: Kg1, Ra1. Black: Kg8, pawns f7 g7 h7. White
ki chaal. Mate in 1 kya hai?

White: Kg1 Ra1

Black: Kg8 f7 g7 h7

Jawab:

✓ Ra8#

-   Back-rank mate: Ra8#.

    6.4.3 Sawal 2: Dimaag mein
    --------------------------

Dimaag mein: knight a1 se h8 tak sabse kam kitni chaal mein pahunchega?

Jawab:

✓ 6

-   6 chaal.
-   Knight lambi doori par dheema hai.

    6.4.4 Sawal 3: Bina board
    -------------------------

Bina board: 1.e4 e5 2.Nf3 Nc6 3.Bb5 ke baad b5 bishop kis black piece ko
attack kar raha hai?

Jawab:

✓ c6 knight

-   Yeh Ruy Lopez hai: bishop c6 knight par.

    6.4.5 Sawal 4: Bina board
    -------------------------

Bina board: 1.e4 e5 2.Nf3 Nc6 3.Bc4 Nf6 4.Ng5 ke baad knight g5 kis black pawn
par attack karta hai?

Jawab:

✓ f7

-   Knight g5 aur bishop c4 dono f7 par: Fried Liver ka idea.

    6.4.6 Sawal 5: Dimaag mein
    --------------------------

Dimaag mein: White bishop c1 kitni chaal mein h6 pahunchega (board khaali)?

Jawab:

✓ 1

-   c1-d2-e3-f4-g5-h6 ek hi diagonal: 1 chaal.

    6.5 Calculation 2: skip rules aur 2 chaal
    =========================================

Kaunsi line chhodni hai, opponent ka forcing jawab, 2 chaal ki line

Yaad rakho

-   Pehle forcing chaalein. Quiet chaal tabhi jab forcing se kuch na mile.
-   Jo chaal bina check/mate ke apna piece latka de, use chhodo.
-   Opponent ke jawab: pehle uske checks aur captures, phir 1 best defence.
-   Har line ek baar socho, nateeja yaad rakho (+3, barabar, mate).
-   Ek line saaf jeet de to baaki chhodo: simple jeet best.
-   Position shaant ho gayi to ruko.

Is lesson ke topics

-   6.5.1 Tez calculation ka raaz: kya NAHI sochna
-   6.5.2 Sawal 1: Tumhari candidate chaal se tumhara rook latak jaata hai,
    aur woh…
-   6.5.3 Sawal 2: Tumne check diya
-   6.5.4 Sawal 3: Ek line mein tum queen jeet rahe ho aur position shaant hai
-   6.5.5 2 chaal ki line kaise sochte hain
-   6.5.6 Abhyaas: 2 chaal mein mate
-   6.5.7 Abhyaas: 2 chaal: check se fork
-   6.5.8 Abhyaas: 2 chaal: opponent ke capture wale jawab ko bhi socho

    6.5.1 Tez calculation ka raaz: kya NAHI sochna
    ----------------------------------------------

-   Grandmaster zyada lines nahi sochte.
-   Woh faltu lines jaldi chhod dete hain.
-   Yeh 6 skip rules yaad karo:
-   1.
-   Pehle forcing chaalein (check, capture, threat).
-   Quiet chaal tabhi jab forcing se kuch na mile.
-   2.
-   Jo chaal bina check ya mate ke apna piece latka de, use chhod do.
-   3.
-   Opponent ke jawab mein pehle uske checks aur captures, phir 1 best
    defence.
-   Baaki jawab chhodo.
-   4.
-   Har line ek hi baar socho, end mein nateeja yaad rakho.
-   Dobara mat socho.
-   5.
-   Ek candidate saaf jeet de raha ho to baaki chhodo.
-   6.
-   Position shaant ho gayi (koi check, capture, threat nahi) to wahin ruko.

    6.5.2 Sawal 1: Tumhari candidate chaal se tumhara rook latak jaata hai, aur woh…
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Tumhari candidate chaal se tumhara rook latak jaata hai, aur woh check bhi
nahi hai. Kya karein?

Jawab:

✓ Is line ko chhod do (skip rule 2)

-   Bina forcing wajah ke piece latkana = line chhodo.
-   Time bacha, dusre candidates par lagao.

    6.5.3 Sawal 2: Tumne check diya
    -------------------------------

Tumne check diya. Opponent ke 3 jawab hain: king hatana, piece beech mein
rakhna, ya checking piece ko capture karna. Pehle kaunsa socho?

Jawab:

✓ Capture (sabse forcing)

-   Opponent ke liye bhi forcing pehle: capture sabse khatarnak jawab hota
    hai.
-   Phir block, phir king move.

    6.5.4 Sawal 3: Ek line mein tum queen jeet rahe ho aur position shaant hai
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Ek line mein tum queen jeet rahe ho aur position shaant hai. Kya aur
candidates sochne chahiye?

Jawab:

✓ Nahi, blunder check karo aur chalo

-   Skip rule 5: saaf jeet mil gayi to bas.
-   Sirf blunder check.

    6.5.5 2 chaal ki line kaise sochte hain
    ---------------------------------------

-   2 chaal = meri chaal → uska jawab → meri chaal.
-   Candidate chuno (CCT).
-   Opponent ka sabse forcing jawab socho. Check ke baad aksar uske 1-2 hi
    jawab hote hain.
-   Har jawab ke baad apni doosri chaal dhundo, aur nateeja gino.
-   Check se shuru hone wali lines sabse tez calculate hoti hain, kyunki
    opponent ke paas kam options hote hain.

    6.5.6 Abhyaas: 2 chaal mein mate

2 chaal mein mate. Check se shuru karo, phir opponent ka jawab socho.

    6.6 Calculation 3: 3-5 chaal ka tree
    ====================================

Candidate tree, checkpoints, board ko dimaag mein rakhna

Yaad rakho

-   Tree: 2-3 candidates, har ek par opponent ke 1-2 forcing jawab.
-   Har 2 chaal par checkpoint: material, king, latke pieces.
-   Hata hua piece dimaag se bhi hatao. Naya khula raasta (file, diagonal)
    yaad rakho.
-   Lambi line sirf forcing chaalon mein. Quiet position mein 2-3 chaal +
    evaluation.
-   Line ke end mein sawal: kya opponent ke paas koi check bacha?

Is lesson ke topics

-   6.6.1 Calculation tree
-   6.6.2 Board ko dimaag mein kaise rakhein
-   6.6.3 Sawal 1: Line mein tumhara bishop capture ho gaya
-   6.6.4 Sawal 2: Position shaant hai, koi check ya capture nahi
-   6.6.5 Abhyaas: 3 chaal: check, check, phir jeet
-   6.6.6 Abhyaas: 3 chaal: pehle sacrifice, phir fork
-   6.6.7 Abhyaas: 4 chaal mein mate

    6.6.1 Calculation tree
    ----------------------

-   3-5 chaal mein lines ped (tree) ki tarah failti hain.
-   Ped ko chhota rakho:
    -   Jad: 2-3 candidates (CCT).
    -   Shaakhaayein: har candidate par opponent ke 1-2 forcing jawab.
    -   Har shaakha ko ek baar end tak le jao, nateeja likho (dimaag mein):
        +3, =, mate.
    -   Sabse achhe nateeje wali jad chuno.
-   Agar 3 candidates × 2 jawab × 2 = 12 lines ho rahi hain to skip rules
    lagao.
-   Aksar 3-4 lines hi asli hoti hain.

    6.6.2 Board ko dimaag mein kaise rakhein
    ----------------------------------------

-   Lambi calculation mein galti aksar visualization ki hoti hai, chess ki
    nahi:
    -   Ghost piece: jo piece capture ho gaya, dimaag mein woh ab bhi "bacha
        raha" lagta hai. Har capture par bolo: "ab yeh piece nahi hai".
    -   Naye raaste: piece hata to uske peeche ki file ya diagonal khul gayi.
        Bolo: "ab d-file khuli".
    -   Checkpoint: har 2 chaal par ruk kar 3 cheezein gino: material, dono
        kings, latke hue pieces.
-   Gym ke Andhe steps yahi sikhate hain: position dekho, yaad karo, phir
    khali board par chalo.

    6.6.3 Sawal 1: Line mein tumhara bishop capture ho gaya
    -------------------------------------------------------

Line mein tumhara bishop capture ho gaya. Ab calculation mein sabse badi galti
kya hogi?

Jawab:

✓ Yeh maan lena ki bishop ab bhi e5 ko bacha raha hai

-   Ghost piece ki galti.
-   Har capture ke baad dimaag ki tasveer update karo.

    6.6.4 Sawal 2: Position shaant hai, koi check ya capture nahi
    -------------------------------------------------------------

Position shaant hai, koi check ya capture nahi. Kitna gehra calculate karein?

Jawab:

✓ 2-3 chaal aur phir evaluation

-   Lambi lines sirf forcing sequences mein.
-   Quiet position mein chhoti calculation + plan + evaluation.

    ######################################################################
    ##  LEVEL 7: MIDDLEGAME 3: CHECKMATE PATTERNS
    ######################################################################

Mate ki kala, back-rank, smothered, aur 25+ famous mate patterns

-   Mate patterns tactics ke "shortcut" hain: shakal pehchaan li to
    calculation turant ho jaati hai.
-   Har pattern ka naam, idea aur practice.

    7.1 Mate patterns: shuruaat aur mate ki kala
    ============================================

Escape squares band karo, quiet moves, famous patterns

Yaad rakho

-   Strong players mate pattern pehchante hain, har baar calculate nahi karte.
-   Back-rank, smothered, Anastasia, Arabian, Boden: shakal yaad karo.
-   Legal's mate: pinned knight se queen sacrifice, teen minor pieces se mate.
-   Greek gift Bxh7+: tab jab f6 par black knight na ho aur Ng5 safe ho.
-   Philidor's legacy: Qg8+!! Rxg8 Nf7# smothered mate.
-   Mate ki kala: pehle king ke escape squares band karo, phir check.
-   Kabhi quiet chaal (bina check) mate se pehle zaroori hoti hai.
-   Escape squares band ho gaye par check nahi to naya check dhundo.

Is lesson ke topics

-   7.1.1 Patterns kyun?
-   7.1.2 Patterns = shortcuts
-   7.1.3 Abhyaas: Smothered mate in 1
-   7.1.4 Abhyaas: Anastasia mate in 1
-   7.1.5 Abhyaas: Arabian mate in 1
-   7.1.6 Abhyaas: Boden mate in 1
-   7.1.7 Abhyaas: Mate in 1
-   7.1.8 Abhyaas: Philidor's legacy (smothered mate)
-   7.1.9 Abhyaas: Greek gift
-   7.1.10 Abhyaas: Mate in 2
-   7.1.11 Abhyaas: Mate in 2
-   7.1.12 Abhyaas: Mate in 2
-   7.1.13 Abhyaas: Mate in 2
-   7.1.14 Abhyaas: Mate in 2

    7.1.1 Patterns kyun?
    --------------------

-   Checkmate pattern = pieces ki jaani-pehchaani shakal jo mate deti hai.
-   Pattern yaad ho to game mein dekhte hi pehchaan loge.
-   Mate ki kala:
-   1.
-   King ke escape squares gino aur unhe ek-ek karke band karo.
-   2.
-   Kabhi quiet chaal (check nahi) loud chaal se behtar: pehle jaal bichhao,
    phir check.
-   3.
-   Saare escape squares band, par check nahi?
-   To naya check dhundo jo un squares ko band rakhe.

    7.1.2 Patterns = shortcuts
    --------------------------

-   Strong players har mate ko calculate nahi karte, woh pattern pehchante
    hain.
-   Yeh patterns yaad karo:
    -   Back-rank mate: king apne pawns ke peeche band.
    -   Smothered mate: king apne hi pieces se ghira, knight mate deta hai.
    -   Anastasia mate: knight + rook, king kinare par.
    -   Arabian mate: rook + knight corner mein.
    -   Boden mate: do bishops criss-cross.
    -   Legal mate: opening trap, queen sacrifice.
    -   Greek gift: Bxh7+ sacrifice castled king par.

    7.2 Back-rank mate
    ==================

Apne hi pawns mein band king

Yaad rakho

-   King back rank par, aage apne pawns: rook ya queen ka check = mate.
-   Bachaav: "luft" (h3/h6 jaisi pawn chaal) se king ko saans do.
-   Defender ko hatao ya deflect karo, phir back rank par ghuso.
-   Famous: Carlsen-So, Bird-Zukertort, Averbakh-Ragozin, Polgar-Bareev,
    McShane-Mirumian, Bernstein-Capablanca.

Is lesson ke topics

-   7.2.1 Back-rank mate
-   7.2.2 Abhyaas: Back rank khula hai
-   7.2.3 Abhyaas: Do attackers vs do defenders, par aakhri capture mate hai!
-   7.2.4 Abhyaas: Back-rank ki kamzori dhundo!
-   7.2.5 Abhyaas: Back-rank ki kamzori dhundo!
-   7.2.6 Abhyaas: Back-rank ki kamzori dhundo!
-   7.2.7 Abhyaas: Back-rank ki kamzori dhundo!
-   7.2.8 Abhyaas: Back-rank ki kamzori dhundo!
-   7.2.9 Abhyaas: Back-rank ki kamzori dhundo!
-   7.2.10 Sawal 1: Back-rank mate se bachne ka sabse simple tareeka?

    7.2.1 Back-rank mate
    --------------------

-   King apni pehli (back) rank par hai aur aage apne hi pawns use rok rahe
    hain.
-   Rook ya queen ka back rank par check = mate.
-   Bachaav: waqt mile to h3/h6 jaisi chaal se king ko luft (saans) do.
-   Hamla: jo piece back rank bachata hai, use deflect ya overload karo.
-   Famous: Carlsen-So, Bird-Zukertort, Orbaan-Kramer, Averbakh-Ragozin
    (1954), Cooper-Ivanov, Shapiro-Silman, Polgar-Bareev, McShane-Mirumian,
    Davidovich-Aguado.

    7.2.2 Abhyaas: Back rank khula hai
    ----------------------------------

Back rank khula hai. Mate in 1.

    8 | . . . . . . k .
    7 | . . . . . p p p
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . P P P
    1 | . R . . . . K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    7.2.10 Sawal 1: Back-rank mate se bachne ka sabse simple tareeka?
    -----------------------------------------------------------------

Back-rank mate se bachne ka sabse simple tareeka?

Jawab:

✓ Waqt mile to ek pawn (h3/h6) chal kar king ko luft dena

-   Luft = king ke liye ek escape square.

    7.3 Smothered mate aur Philidor's legacy
    ========================================

Knight ka mate jab king apne pieces mein dab jaaye

Yaad rakho

-   Smothered mate: king ke saare squares apne pieces se bhare, knight check
    deta hai.
-   Philidor's legacy: queen check, knight checks, queen sacrifice g8 par,
    phir Nf7#.
-   Suffocation (semi-smothered) mate: knight + doosra piece, king apne pieces
    mein phansa.
-   Famous: McConnell-Morphy, Morphy vs NN (1856), Greco (1620), Morphy-Bryan
    (1859).

Is lesson ke topics

-   7.3.1 Smothered mate
-   7.3.2 Abhyaas: Smothered mate in 1!
-   7.3.3 Abhyaas: Doosre corner mein smothered mate
-   7.3.4 Abhyaas: Philidor's legacy (chhota version)
-   7.3.5 Suffocation mate (semi-smothered)
-   7.3.6 Sawal 1: Smothered mate kis piece se hota hai?

    7.3.1 Smothered mate
    --------------------

-   King ke saare aas-paas ke squares apne hi pieces se bhare hain, aur knight
    check deta hai (knight ka check block nahi hota).
-   Sabse sundar mate!
-   Famous: McConnell-Morphy, Morphy vs NN (New Orleans 1856), NN-Greco (1620,
    1625), Bird/Dobell vs NN, Bischoff-Mueller, Morphy-Bryan (1859),
    Hendriks-Welling.

    7.3.2 Abhyaas: Smothered mate in 1!
    -----------------------------------

Smothered mate in 1!

    8 | . . . . . . r k
    7 | . . . . . . p p
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . N .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . . . .
    1 | . . . . . . K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h
