    7.3.5 Suffocation mate (semi-smothered)
    ---------------------------------------

-   Knight check deta hai, king ke kuch squares apne pieces se band, baaki
    kisi doosre piece (jaise bishop) se.
-   King "dam ghut-ne" se marta hai.

    7.3.6 Sawal 1: Smothered mate kis piece se hota hai?
    ----------------------------------------------------

Smothered mate kis piece se hota hai?

Jawab:

✓ Knight

-   Sirf knight, kyunki uska check block nahi hota.

    7.4 Rook aur knight/bishop ke mate patterns
    ===========================================

Anastasia, Arabian, corner, Morphy, Pillsbury, Mayet, Opera, blind swine,
Reti, hook, Vukovic

Yaad rakho

-   Anastasia: knight e7 + rook h-file, king h7.
-   Arabian: rook h7 + knight f6, king h8.
-   Corner: rook g-file + knight f7, king h8 (pawn h7).
-   Morphy: bishop long diagonal se check, rook g-file band.
-   Pillsbury: rook g-file se check, bishop h8 band karta hai.
-   Mayet: rook check, door se bishop support.
-   Opera: rook back rank par, bishop support (Morphy ka Opera game).
-   Blind swine: do rooks 7th rank par.
-   Reti: bishop + rook, king apne pieces mein (Reti-Tartakower).
-   Hook: rook + knight + pawn ek chain. Vukovic: rook + knight, king kinare
    par.

Is lesson ke topics

-   7.4.1 Rook + minor piece patterns
-   7.4.2 Abhyaas: Anastasia's mate
-   7.4.3 Abhyaas: Arabian mate
-   7.4.4 Abhyaas: Corner mate
-   7.4.5 Abhyaas: Morphy's mate
-   7.4.6 Abhyaas: Pillsbury's mate
-   7.4.7 Abhyaas: Mayet's mate
-   7.4.8 Abhyaas: Opera mate
-   7.4.9 Abhyaas: Blind swine mate (mate in 3)

    7.4.1 Rook + minor piece patterns
    ---------------------------------

-   Anastasia: knight e7 (g6, g8 band) + rook h-file par check, king h7.
    (Milosavljevic-Rakic)
-   Arabian: rook h7 + knight f6, king h8. Sabse purana pattern.
    (Gunsberg-Janowski)
-   Corner mate: rook g-file band kare, knight f7 se check, king h8 (pawn h7).
    (Myers-Poliakoff)
-   Morphy: bishop long diagonal se check, rook g-file band.
-   Pillsbury: ulta: rook g-file se check, bishop h8 band.
-   Mayet: rook check, door se bishop support (killer common square ka type).
-   Opera: rook back rank par check, bishop support. (Morphy ka Opera game)
-   Blind swine: do rooks 7th rank par ("pigs"), king ko maarte hain.
-   Reti: bishop + rook, king apne pieces mein (Reti-Tartakower).
-   Hook: rook ko knight protect kare, knight ko pawn: ek chain.
    (Marshall-Napier)
-   Vukovic: rook + knight, king kinare par. (Staunton-Williams)

    7.4.2 Abhyaas: Anastasia's mate
    -------------------------------

Anastasia's mate: mate karo!

    8 | . . . . . . . .
    7 | . . . . N . p k
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . R . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . . . .
    1 | K . . . . . . .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

Line ke baad position

    7.5 Queen, bishop aur pawn ke mate patterns
    ===========================================

Damiano, Greco, Lolli, dovetail, swallow's tail, epaulette, Boden, Blackburne,
double bishop, Max Lange, kill box, pawn mate

Yaad rakho

-   Damiano: queen h7 par, pawn g6 support.
-   Greco: bishop g8 band kare, queen/rook h-file par mate.
-   Lolli: pawn f6 + queen g7 (thorn pawn).
-   Dovetail: queen diagonal se, king ke peeche ke squares apne pieces se
    band.
-   Swallow's tail (Gueridon): queen seedha saamne, king ke diagonal peeche
    apne rooks.
-   Epaulette: king ke dono taraf apne rooks (kandhe), queen saamne se.
-   Boden: do bishops crisscross (a6 + f4 jaisi diagonals).
-   Blackburne: do bishops + knight. Double bishop: do bishops saath diagonals
    par.
-   Max Lange: queen + bishop. Kill box: queen + rook ka box. Pawn mate (David
    & Goliath): pawn ka mate.

Is lesson ke topics

-   7.5.1 Queen, bishop aur pawn patterns
-   7.5.2 Abhyaas: Damiano's mate
-   7.5.3 Abhyaas: Greco's mate
-   7.5.4 Abhyaas: Lolli's mate (thorn pawn)
-   7.5.5 Abhyaas: Dovetail mate
-   7.5.6 Abhyaas: Swallow's tail mate
-   7.5.7 Abhyaas: Epaulette mate
-   7.5.8 Abhyaas: Boden's mate
-   7.5.9 Abhyaas: Blackburne's mate
-   7.5.10 Abhyaas: Pawn mate (David & Goliath)

    7.5.1 Queen, bishop aur pawn patterns
    -------------------------------------

-   Damiano: queen h7 par mate, pawn g6 support.
-   Greco: bishop c4 ne g8 band kiya, queen/rook h-file par mate.
    (Short-Sokolov)
-   Lolli (thorn pawn): pawn f6 + queen g7. (Blackburne-Steinkuehler)
-   Dovetail: queen diagonal se check, king ke peeche ke do squares apne
    pieces se band.
-   Swallow's tail / Gueridon: queen seedha saamne (protected), king ke
    diagonal peeche apne rooks.
-   Epaulette: king ke dono kandhon par apne rooks, queen saamne se.
    (Carlsen-Ernst)
-   Boden: do bishops crisscross diagonals par. (Canal vs NN)
-   Blackburne: do bishops + knight.
-   Double bishop: do bishops paas-paas diagonals par.
-   Max Lange: queen + bishop ka chatur kaam.
-   Kill box: queen + rook ek box banate hain.
-   Queen's mate, Rook (box) mate: Level 3 mein seekhe.
-   Pawn mate (David & Goliath): chhota sa pawn mate deta hai.
-   Legal's mate: Level 4 ke traps mein seekha.

Chaal ke baad

    ######################################################################
    ##  LEVEL 8: MIDDLEGAME 4: POSITION AUR PAWN STRUCTURE
    ######################################################################

Position ke elements, exchanges, pawn structure, weak squares, outposts

-   Jab koi tactic na ho, tab kya karein?
-   Pehle position ke elements aur pawn structure samjho: yahi plan ki neev
    hai.

    8.1 Position ke elements aur exchanges
    ======================================

Material, time, space, king safety, pawn structure; kab trade karein

Yaad rakho

-   Material sab kuch nahi: time (development), space, king safety, pawn
    structure bhi ginti mein.
-   Jeet rahe ho (material aage): pieces trade karo, pawns nahi.
-   Haar rahe ho: pawns trade karo, pieces nahi (draw ke chances).
-   King par heavy attack: attacking pieces trade karo.
-   Tang (cramped) position: pieces trade karke saans lo, bure piece ko trade
    karo.

Is lesson ke topics

-   8.1.1 Position ke 5 elements
-   8.1.2 Material sab kuch nahi: Ed. Lasker vs Thomas (1912)
-   8.1.3 Exchanges kab karein
-   8.1.4 Sawal 1: Tum ek rook aage ho
-   8.1.5 Sawal 2: Tum ek pawn peeche ho, endgame aa raha hai
-   8.1.6 Sawal 3: Opponent ki queen, rook aur knight tumhare king par attack
    kar…
-   8.1.7 Sawal 4: Tumhari position tang hai aur ek bishop apne pawns ke
    peeche band…

    8.1.1 Position ke 5 elements
    ----------------------------

-   Sirf "accountant" ki tarah material mat gino.
-   Dekho:
-   1.
-   Material (points)
-   2.
-   Time (development, tempo)
-   3.
-   Space (kitni jagah tumhare pieces ke paas)
-   4.
-   King safety
-   5.
-   Pawn structure
-   Ek element mein aage hokar doosre mein peeche ho sakte ho.
-   Jaise gambit: material kam, time zyada.

    8.1.2 Material sab kuch nahi: Ed. Lasker vs Thomas (1912)
    ---------------------------------------------------------

-   Queen sacrifice aur phir Black king ka poore board par shikaar!

1. d4 e6 2. Nf3 f5 3. Nc3 Nf6 4. Bg5 Be7 5. Bxf6 Bxf6 6. e4 fxe4 7. Nxe4 b6
8. Ne5 O-O 9. Bd3 Bb7 10. Qh5 Qe7 11. Qxh7+ Kxh7 12. Nxf6+ Kh6 13. Neg4+ Kg5
14. h4+ Kf4 15. g3+ Kf3 16. Be2+ Kg2 17. Rh2+ Kg1 18. Kd2#

-   5.Bxf6 bishop dekar light squares par kabza.
-   10.Qh5 attack.
-   11.Qxh7+!! queen sacrifice.
-   12.Nxf6+ double check!
-   14.h4+ king ko aage kheencho.
-   18.Kd2# king ki chaal se mate (O-O-O# bhi tha)!

    8 | r n . q . r k .
    7 | p b p p . . p p
    6 | . p . . p b . .
    5 | . . . . N . . Q

Final position (18.Kd2#)

    8.1.3 Exchanges kab karein
    --------------------------

-   Material aage: pieces trade karo, pawns nahi. Kam pieces = tumhara extra
    material zyada bhaari. (Benko vs Fischer)
-   Material peeche: pawns trade karo, pieces nahi. Kam pawns = draw ke
    chances (jaise sirf bishop bacha to mate nahi).
-   King par heavy attack: attacking pieces trade karo, attack khatam.
-   Tang (cramped) position: trade karke jagah banao, khaas kar apna bura
    piece trade karo.

    8.1.4 Sawal 1: Tum ek rook aage ho
    ----------------------------------

Tum ek rook aage ho. Opponent queen trade offer karta hai. Kya karoge?

Jawab:

✓ Trade karo: pieces kam = jeet aasaan

-   Aage ho to pieces trade.

    8.1.5 Sawal 2: Tum ek pawn peeche ho, endgame aa raha hai
    ---------------------------------------------------------

Tum ek pawn peeche ho, endgame aa raha hai. Kya trade karna achha?

Jawab:

✓ Pawns

-   Pawns kam honge to draw ke chances badhenge.

    8.1.6 Sawal 3: Opponent ki queen, rook aur knight tumhare king par attack kar…
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Opponent ki queen, rook aur knight tumhare king par attack kar rahe hain.
Achha idea?

Jawab:

✓ Attacking pieces ko trade karo

-   Attackers trade = attack khatam.

    8.1.7 Sawal 4: Tumhari position tang hai aur ek bishop apne pawns ke peeche band…
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Tumhari position tang hai aur ek bishop apne pawns ke peeche band (bura).
Plan?

Jawab:

✓ Bure bishop ko trade karne ki koshish

-   Cramped position mein trades se raahat.

    8.2 Pawn structure
    ==================

Isolated, doubled, backward, passed, chains

Yaad rakho

-   Pawn moves permanent hain: structure decide karta hai pieces kahan achhe.
-   IQP: kamzor pawn, par active pieces. IQP wala attack kare, doosra trade
    kare.
-   Backward pawn ke aage ka square ek hole banta hai.
-   Pawn chain: base par attack karo (Nimzowitsch).
-   "Pawns chess ki aatma hain" (Philidor).
-   Pawns peeche nahi jaate: har pawn chaal ek pakka faisla. Socho kaunse
    squares hamesha ke liye kamzor honge.
-   Center ko pawns se bharna zaroori nahi: pieces se control bhi chalega
    (hypermodern, Reti vs Capablanca).
-   Pawn kamzor tab hota hai jab use pawn se bachaya na ja sake (dependency
    pieces par).
-   Pieces aur pawns ka harmony: bishop apne pawns ke opposite colour par.

Is lesson ke topics

-   8.2.1 Pawns: chess ki aatma
-   8.2.2 Pawns = position ki haddi
-   8.2.3 Isolated pawn (IQP)
-   8.2.4 Doubled aur backward pawns
-   8.2.5 Pawn chain
-   8.2.6 Sawal 1: Isolated pawn ke saamne wala square kis kaam ka hai?
-   8.2.7 Sawal 2: Pawn chain par attack kahan karna chahiye?
-   8.2.8 Sawal 3: Tumhare center pawns light squares par hain
-   8.2.9 Sawal 4: Pawn chaal ko "irreversible" kyun kehte hain?

    8.2.1 Pawns: chess ki aatma
    ---------------------------

-   "Pawns are the soul of chess" (Philidor).
-   Pawn structure (formation) batata hai ki pieces kahan jaayenge aur plan
    kya hoga.
-   Pawns peeche nahi jaate: har pawn chaal ek pakka faisla hai. Plaskett vs
    Adams aur Fischer vs Addison (probing strategy) mein galat pawn chaalon se
    hamesha ki kamzori bani.
-   Center bharna zaroori nahi: Reti vs Capablanca: pieces se door se center
    control (hypermodern).
-   Kamzor pawn = jise pawn se bachaya na ja sake, pieces par nirbhar.
-   Harmony: pieces aur pawns saath kaam karein. Bishop apne pawns ke opposite
    colour par. (Karjakin-Adams, Kasparov-Adams, Nimzowitsch-Capablanca)
-   Har structure ke upside aur downside hote hain: dono khelkar dekho.

    8.2.2 Pawns = position ki haddi
    -------------------------------

-   "Pawns are the soul of chess" (Philidor).
-   Pawns peeche nahi jaate, isliye har pawn move permanent hai.
-   Pawn structure decide karta hai pieces kahan achhe honge.

    8.2.3 Isolated pawn (IQP)
    -------------------------

-   d4 pawn ke bagal (c, e files) mein koi apna pawn nahi: isolated.
-   Kamzori: pawn se protect nahi hota, aur uske aage d5 square blockade ke
    liye perfect (Black knight d5 par).
-   Taakat: active pieces, d5 break aur kingside attack.
-   IQP wala attack kare, doosra pieces trade karke endgame mein jaaye.

    8 | r . b q . r k .
    7 | p p . . b p p p
    6 | . . n . p n . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . P . . . .
    3 | . . N B . N . .
    2 | P P . . . P P P
    1 | R . B Q . R K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    8.2.4 Doubled aur backward pawns
    --------------------------------

-   Backward pawn: bagal ke pawns aage nikal gaye, aur yeh aage nahi badh
    sakta kyunki aage ka square dushman control karta hai.
-   Yahan Black ka d6 backward hai aur d5 ek hole hai: White ke knight ke liye
    perfect ghar.
-   Doubled pawns (ek file par do): aksar kamzor, par open file deta hai.

    8 | r . b q k b . r
    7 | . p . . . p p p
    6 | p . n p . n . .
    5 | . . . . p . . .
    4 | . . . . P . . .
    3 | . N N . . . . .
    2 | P P P . B P P P
    1 | R . B Q K . . R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    8.2.5 Pawn chain
    ----------------

-   Pawn chain d4-e5 (White) aur e6-d5 (Black).
-   Rule (Nimzowitsch): chain ki base par attack karo.
-   Black ...c5 se d4 (base) par vaar karta hai.
-   White kingside par (f4-f5) attack karta hai, kyunki chain ka sira us taraf
    ishaara karta hai.

    8 | r n b q k b n r
    7 | p p . . . p p p
    6 | . . . . p . . .
    5 | . . p p P . . .
    4 | . . . P . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | P P P . . P P P
    1 | R N B Q K B N R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    8.2.6 Sawal 1: Isolated pawn ke saamne wala square kis kaam ka hai?
    -------------------------------------------------------------------

Isolated pawn ke saamne wala square kis kaam ka hai?

Jawab:

✓ Dushman ke knight ke liye blockade ghar

    8.2.7 Sawal 2: Pawn chain par attack kahan karna chahiye?
    ---------------------------------------------------------

Pawn chain par attack kahan karna chahiye?

Jawab:

✓ Chain ki base par

    8.2.8 Sawal 3: Tumhare center pawns light squares par hain
    ----------------------------------------------------------

Tumhare center pawns light squares par hain. Kaunsa bishop "good" hai?

Jawab:

✓ Dark-square bishop

-   Apne pawns ke opposite colour wala bishop free hota hai.

    8.2.9 Sawal 4: Pawn chaal ko "irreversible" kyun kehte hain?
    ------------------------------------------------------------

Pawn chaal ko "irreversible" kyun kehte hain?

Jawab:

✓ Pawn peeche nahi jaata, jo square chhoda woh hamesha ke liye

-   Isliye pawn chaal soch-samajh kar.

    8.3 Pawn structure: gehraai se
    ==============================

Pawn breaks, holes, minority attack, thorn pawn, passed, doubled, IQP,
backward, hanging: upside aur downside

Yaad rakho

-   Pawn break (lever): pawn se dushman ki chain par chot karke lines kholna.
-   Hole: aisa square jise pawn kabhi control nahi kar sakta. Uske guard
    bishop ko trade karo.
-   Minority attack: kam pawns (jaise a+b) se zyada pawns (a+b+c) par hamla
    karke kamzori banana.
-   Thorn pawn: dushman king ke paas gehra ghusa pawn (f6/h6): mate ke mauke.
-   Har structure ke dono pehlu: doubled, IQP, backward, hanging pawns kamzor
    bhi, strong bhi.

Is lesson ke topics

-   8.3.1 Pawn break aur hole
-   8.3.2 Minority attack
-   8.3.3 Sawal 1: Minority attack ka maksad?
-   8.3.4 Thorn pawn
-   8.3.5 Structures: upside aur downside
-   8.3.6 Sawal 2: Doubled pawns ka ek upside?

    8.3.1 Pawn break aur hole
    -------------------------

-   Pawn break (lever): apne pawn se dushman ke pawn par chot karke lines
    kholna. Jaise French mein ...c5, King's Indian mein ...f5.
-   Structural hole: aisa square jise koi pawn kabhi control nahi kar sakta
    (jaise d5 jab c aur e pawns aage chale gaye). Agar us square ka guard ek
    bishop hai, use trade karo: hole tumhara.

    8.3.2 Minority attack
    ---------------------

-   Queen's Gambit Exchange structure: White ke 2 pawns (a, b) Black ke 3
    pawns (a, b, c) par chadhte hain: b4-b5.
-   Trade ke baad Black ke paas kamzor c-pawn (backward/isolated) bachta hai.

    8 | r . b q . r k .
    7 | p p . . b p p p
    6 | . . n . p n . .
    5 | . . . p . . . .
    4 | . P . P . . . .
    3 | . . N . P N . .
    2 | P . . . B P P P
    1 | R . . Q . R K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    8.3.3 Sawal 1: Minority attack ka maksad?
    -----------------------------------------

Minority attack ka maksad?

Jawab:

✓ Dushman ke structure mein kamzor pawn banana

-   Kamzor pawn = lambe samay ka target.

    8.3.4 Thorn pawn
    ----------------

-   Dushman king ke paas gehra ghusa pawn, jaise f6 ya h6 (Black castled king
    ke against).
-   Yeh g7 jaise squares ko control karta hai aur mate ke patterns (Lolli)
    aasaan banata hai.
-   Jeet ke chances bahut badh jaate hain.
-   Examples: Lorenz-Orlov, Kramnik-Anand, Morales-Sanchez, Ivanovic-Popovic.

    8 | . . . . . . k .
    7 | . . . . . p . p
    6 | . . . . . P p .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . . . .
    1 | . . . . . . K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    8.3.5 Structures: upside aur downside
    -------------------------------------

-   Passed pawn: jeet ke chances, "pushed" hone chahiye. (Torre vs NN)
-   Doubled pawns: kamzor (ek doosre ko bacha nahi sakte), par open file aur
    center control de sakte hain. (Botvinnik vs Levenfish)
-   IQP (isolated queen's pawn): kamzor endgame mein, par middlegame mein
    activity aur attack.
-   Backward pawn: aage ka square hole ban jaata hai, par kabhi woh square aur
    pawn solid defense bhi.
-   Hanging pawns (c + d saath, bagal mein koi nahi): aage badhkar dabaav, par
    target bhi.

    8.3.6 Sawal 2: Doubled pawns ka ek upside?
    ------------------------------------------

Doubled pawns ka ek upside?

Jawab:

✓ Saath mein ek open/semi-open file milti hai

-   Capture se file khulti hai: rook ke liye.

    8.4 Weak squares aur outposts
    =============================

Pieces ke liye ghar

Yaad rakho

-   Outpost = jahan dushman pawn tumhare piece ko bhaga na sake.
-   Outpost par knight rook jitna strong ho sakta hai.
-   Bad bishop: apne hi pawns usi color par. Pawns bishop ke ulte color par
    rakho.

Is lesson ke topics

-   8.4.1 Outpost
-   8.4.2 Good bishop vs bad bishop
-   8.4.3 Sawal 1: Knight ke liye sabse achha square?
-   8.4.4 Sawal 2: Outpost ki sabse zaroori shart kya hai?
-   8.4.5 Sawal 3: White ka bishop (c1) good hai ya bad?
-   8.4.6 Sawal 4: Knight ke liye sabse achha square kaunsa hai?

    8.4.1 Outpost
    -------------

-   Outpost: aisa square jahan tumhara piece baith sake aur koi dushman pawn
    use bhaga na sake, aur aksar apne pawn se protected ho.
-   d5 par knight: Black ke c aur e pawns use kabhi attack nahi kar sakte.
-   Aisa knight ek rook jitna strong ho sakta hai!
-   Weak square = jo pawns se protect na ho sake.
-   Pawn move karte waqt socho: kaunse squares kamzor ho rahe hain?

    8 | r . . . . r k .
    7 | . p . . . p p p
    6 | p . . p . . . .
    5 | . . . N p . . .
    4 | . . . . P . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | P P P . . P P P
    1 | R . . . . R K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    8.4.2 Good bishop vs bad bishop
    -------------------------------

-   White ke pawns light squares par hain (c4, d5, e4), aur bishop bhi
    light-square ka hai: woh apne hi pawns se ruka hai = bad bishop.
-   Rule: pawns ko apne bishop ke ulte color par rakho.
-   Bad bishop ko trade karo ya pawns ke bahar laao.

    8 | . . . . k . . .
    7 | . . . . . . . .
    6 | . . . p . . . .
    5 | . . p P p . . .
    4 | . . P . P . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . B . . .
    1 | . . . . K . . .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    8.4.3 Sawal 1: Knight ke liye sabse achha square?
    -------------------------------------------------

Knight ke liye sabse achha square?

Jawab:

✓ Center mein outpost jahan pawn bhaga na sake

    8.4.4 Sawal 2: Outpost ki sabse zaroori shart kya hai?
    ------------------------------------------------------

Outpost ki sabse zaroori shart kya hai?

Jawab:

✓ Koi dushman pawn us piece ko bhaga na sake

-   Outpost = jahan dushman pawn kabhi attack na kar sake.
-   Upar se apna pawn protect kare to aur achha.

    8.4.5 Sawal 3: White ka bishop (c1) good hai ya bad?
    ----------------------------------------------------

White ka bishop (c1) good hai ya bad?

        a b c d e f g h

Jawab:

✓ Good bishop

-   White ke pawns light squares par hain, bishop dark squares ka hai: pawns
    raasta nahi rokte = good bishop.

    8.4.6 Sawal 4: Knight ke liye sabse achha square kaunsa hai?
    ------------------------------------------------------------

Knight ke liye sabse achha square kaunsa hai?

    8 | r . . . . r k .
    7 | . p . . . p p p
    6 | p . . p . . . .

Jawab:

✓ d5 (outpost, e4 se protected)

-   d5 par koi black pawn attack nahi kar sakta (c6/e6 pawn nahi) aur e4 pawn
    support karta hai.
-   "Knight on the rim is dim."

    ######################################################################
    ##  LEVEL 9: MIDDLEGAME 5: PLANS AUR POSITIONAL KHEL
    ######################################################################

Open files, 7th rank, plans, prophylaxis, positional sacrifices

-   Ab position ko plan mein badlo: rooks ko kaam do, plan banao, opponent ke
    plan roko, aur kabhi position ke liye material do.

    9.1 Open files, 7th rank, piece activity
    ========================================

Rooks aur pieces ko zinda karo

Yaad rakho

-   Rooks ko open / half-open files par lao, phir 7th rank par ghuso.
-   Do rooks 7th par ("pigs on the 7th") aksar jeet.
-   Golden rule: sabse kharab piece ko behtar karo.

Is lesson ke topics

-   9.1.1 Rooks ko kaam do
-   9.1.2 Sawal 1: Koi tactic nahi, koi threat nahi
-   9.1.3 Sawal 2: Rook ke liye sabse achhi file kaunsi hai?
-   9.1.4 Sawal 3: Rook 7th rank par itna strong kyun hota hai?
-   9.1.5 Sawal 4: Do rooks ek hi open file par (doubled rooks)
-   9.1.6 Diagram: Rook open file se 7th rank par ("pig on the 7th")

    9.1.1 Rooks ko kaam do
    ----------------------

-   Rooks ko open file (jisme koi pawn nahi) par lao, ya half-open file par.
-   File kabze ke baad 7th rank par ghuso: wahan pawns aur king dono atakte
    hain. Do rooks 7th par = "pigs on the 7th", aksar jeet.
-   Doubling: do rooks ek file par.
-   General rule: sabse kharab piece ko behtar karo.
-   Har position mein socho: mera kaunsa piece kuch nahi kar raha?

    8 | . . . . . . k .
    7 | # R # # # p p p
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . P P P
    1 | . . . . . . K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    9.1.2 Sawal 1: Koi tactic nahi, koi threat nahi
    -----------------------------------------------

Koi tactic nahi, koi threat nahi. Achha general plan?

Jawab:

✓ Sabse kharab piece ko behtar square par lao

    9.1.3 Sawal 2: Rook ke liye sabse achhi file kaunsi hai?
    --------------------------------------------------------

Rook ke liye sabse achhi file kaunsi hai?

    8 | . . . r . . k .

Jawab:

✓ d-file (koi pawn nahi = open)

-   Rook ko open file chahiye.
-   Wahan se woh 7th/8th rank tak ghus sakta hai.

    9.1.4 Sawal 3: Rook 7th rank par itna strong kyun hota hai?
    -----------------------------------------------------------

Rook 7th rank par itna strong kyun hota hai?

Jawab:

✓ Wahan pawns aur king dono par dabaav hota hai

-   7th rank par opponent ke pawns ghar par hote hain aur king 8th rank par
    fansta hai: "rook on the 7th" = double nishaana.

    9.1.5 Sawal 4: Do rooks ek hi open file par (doubled rooks)
    -----------------------------------------------------------

Do rooks ek hi open file par (doubled rooks). Fayda?

Jawab:

✓ File par dabaav double, aur file ka kabza pakka

-   Doubled rooks file jeet lete hain.
-   Phir ek rook aage 7th rank par ghus sakta hai.

    9.2 Plan kaise banayein
    =======================

Imbalances aur attack

Yaad rakho

-   Plan imbalances se banta hai: material, minor pieces, pawn structure,
    space, files, king safety.
-   Jahan strong ho wahan khelo.
-   Opposite side castling = pawn storm race; same side castling mein
    center/queenside khelo.
-   Minority attack (b4-b5): Carlsbad structure mein c6 par kamzor pawn
    banana.
-   Piece activity: har piece ko kaam do; bure piece ko behtar square par lao.

Is lesson ke topics

-   9.2.1 Imbalances dekho
-   9.2.2 Opposite side castling = pawn storm
-   9.2.3 Minority attack
-   9.2.4 Sawal 1: Tumhare paas kingside mein zyada space hai aur kings alag
    taraf…
-   9.2.5 Sawal 2: Plan banane ka pehla kadam?
-   9.2.6 Sawal 3: Minority attack (b4-b5) ka maksad kya hai?
-   9.2.7 Sawal 4: Kings alag taraf castled hain (opposite side)

    9.2.1 Imbalances dekho
    ----------------------

-   Plan banane ke liye farq (imbalances) dekho:
    -   Material: kaun aage?
    -   Minor pieces: bishop vs knight, bishop pair?
    -   Pawn structure: kamzor pawns, passed pawns, majorities
    -   Space: kiske paas zyada jagah?
    -   Open files / weak squares
    -   Development aur king safety
-   Phir: jahan tum strong ho wahan khelo.
-   Space zyada hai to us taraf attack karo.
-   Kamzori hai to use theek karo ya trades se bacho.

    9.2.2 Opposite side castling = pawn storm
    -----------------------------------------

-   Jab kings alag-alag taraf castle karein, to dono apne pawns dushman king
    ki taraf daudaate hain (pawn storm).
-   Yahan speed sab kuch hai: jo pehle file kholega woh jeetega.
-   Same side castle ho to pawns aage badhana apne king ko kamzor karta hai:
    wahan center ya queenside mein khelo.

    8 | . . k r . . . r
    7 | p p p . . p p p
    6 | . . n . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . N . .
    2 | P P P . . P P P
    1 | . . K R . . . R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    9.2.3 Minority attack
    ---------------------

-   Queen's Gambit structure (Carlsbad): White kam pawns wali side (queenside:
    a, b vs a, b, c) se attack karta hai: b4-b5.
-   Maksad: c6 par ek kamzor pawn banana.
-   Yeh dikhata hai: plan pawn structure se aata hai.

    8 | . . . . k . . .
    7 | p p . . . p p p
    6 | . . p . . . . .
    5 | . . . p . . . .
    4 | . . . P . . . .
    3 | . . . . P . . .
    2 | P P . . . P P P
    1 | . . . . K . . .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    9.2.4 Sawal 1: Tumhare paas kingside mein zyada space hai aur kings alag taraf…
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Tumhare paas kingside mein zyada space hai aur kings alag taraf castled hain.
Plan?

Jawab:

✓ Kingside pawn storm aur attack

    9.2.5 Sawal 2: Plan banane ka pehla kadam?
    ------------------------------------------

Plan banane ka pehla kadam?

Jawab:

✓ Imbalances (farq) dekhna: material, pawns, pieces, king safety, space

-   Plan farq se aata hai.
-   Pehle dekho kis cheez mein tum aage ho, phir us farq ko badhao.

    9.2.6 Sawal 3: Minority attack (b4-b5) ka maksad kya hai?
    ---------------------------------------------------------

Minority attack (b4-b5) ka maksad kya hai?

Jawab:

✓ Black ke queenside par ek kamzor pawn (jaise c6) banana

-   Kam pawns se zyada pawns par attack karke unki structure todo, phir kamzor
    pawn ko jeeto.

    9.2.7 Sawal 4: Kings alag taraf castled hain (opposite side)
    ------------------------------------------------------------

Kings alag taraf castled hain (opposite side). Sabse zaroori kya hai?

Jawab:

✓ Speed: pehle file kholna aur attack

-   Opposite side castling mein race hoti hai.
-   Jo pehle attack khole, woh aksar jeetta hai.

    9.3 Prophylaxis aur restriction
    ===============================

Opponent ka plan pehle roko

Yaad rakho

-   Har chaal se pehle: "Opponent kya chahta hai?" Use pehle roko.
-   Misaal: h3 (...Bg4 roko), Kh1 (checks se pehle hi bacho).
-   Restriction: dushman pieces ke achhe squares cheen lo.

Is lesson ke topics

-   9.3.1 Prophylaxis
-   9.3.2 Sawal 1: Ruy Lopez mein White h3 kyun khelta hai?
-   9.3.3 Sawal 2: Prophylaxis ka pehla sawal kya hai?
-   9.3.4 Sawal 3: Castle ke baad h3 (luft) kyun khelte hain?
-   9.3.5 Sawal 4: Opponent ka knight d4 outpost par aana chahta hai

    9.3.1 Prophylaxis
    -----------------

-   Prophylaxis (Nimzowitsch, Karpov, Petrosian): apni chaal se pehle poocho
    "Opponent kya karna chahta hai?" aur use pehle hi rok do.
-   h3 khelna taaki ...Bg4 pin na ho
-   Rook ko us file par rakhna jo khulne wali hai
-   King ko pehle hi safe square (Kh1) par le jaana taaki baad mein checks na
    aayein
-   Restriction: dushman pieces ke achhe squares cheen lo.
-   Jo piece hil nahi sakta, woh piece nahi.

    9.3.2 Sawal 1: Ruy Lopez mein White h3 kyun khelta hai?
    -------------------------------------------------------

Ruy Lopez mein White h3 kyun khelta hai?

Jawab:

✓ Black bishop ko g4 aane se rokne ke liye (prophylaxis)

    9.3.3 Sawal 2: Prophylaxis ka pehla sawal kya hai?
    --------------------------------------------------

Prophylaxis ka pehla sawal kya hai?

Jawab:

✓ Opponent kya karna chahta hai?

-   Pehle opponent ka plan samjho, phir use shuru hone se pehle hi roko.

    9.3.4 Sawal 3: Castle ke baad h3 (luft) kyun khelte hain?
    ---------------------------------------------------------

Castle ke baad h3 (luft) kyun khelte hain?

Jawab:

✓ Back-rank mate se bachne ke liye king ko saans dena

-   Luft king ko ek khaali square deta hai, taaki back rank par ek check mate
    na ban jaaye.

    9.3.5 Sawal 4: Opponent ka knight d4 outpost par aana chahta hai
    ----------------------------------------------------------------

Opponent ka knight d4 outpost par aana chahta hai. Restriction ka tareeka?

Jawab:

✓ Apne pawn (jaise c3 ya e3) se d4 ko cover kar do

-   Restriction = opponent ke achhe squares pehle hi band kar do, khaaskar
    pawn se.

    9.4 Positional sacrifices
    =========================

Material dekar position lena

Yaad rakho

-   Exchange sac (rook dekar minor piece) position ke liye: Dragon ka
    ...Rxc3!.
-   Kab: dushman king khule, dominant piece mile, ya dushman rook bekaar ho.
-   Gambit = pawn dekar development / initiative.

Is lesson ke topics

-   9.4.1 Exchange sacrifice
-   9.4.2 Sawal 1: Positional sacrifice ka maksad?
-   9.4.3 Sawal 2: Sicilian Dragon mein Black ...Rxc3 kyun khelta hai?
-   9.4.4 Sawal 3: Exchange sacrifice mein tum kya dete ho aur kya lete ho?
-   9.4.5 Sawal 4: Gambit (pawn sacrifice) kab theek hai?

    9.4.1 Exchange sacrifice
    ------------------------

-   Exchange sacrifice: rook (5) dekar minor piece (3) lena, position ke liye.
-   Sicilian Dragon mein ...Rxc3! famous hai: White ke king ke saamne pawns
    toot jaate hain, aur e4 kamzor hota hai.
-   Kab karein: jab sacrifice se dushman king khul jaaye, ya tumhe dominant
    piece aur pawns milein, ya dushman ka rook kaam ka na ho.
-   Pawn sacrifice (gambit) se bhi development, open lines ya initiative milta
    hai.

    8 | . . r . . r k .
    7 | p p . b p p b p
    6 | . . . p . n p .
    5 | q . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . N P . . .
    3 | . B N . B P . .
    2 | P P P Q . . P P
    1 | . . K R . . . R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    9.4.2 Sawal 1: Positional sacrifice ka maksad?
    ----------------------------------------------

Positional sacrifice ka maksad?

Jawab:

✓ Lambe samay ka fayda: king safety tootna, strong pieces, initiative

    9.4.3 Sawal 2: Sicilian Dragon mein Black ...Rxc3 kyun khelta hai?
    ------------------------------------------------------------------

Sicilian Dragon mein Black ...Rxc3 kyun khelta hai?

    8 | . . r . . r k .
    7 | p p . b p p b p
    6 | . . . p . n p .
    5 | q . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . N P . . .
    3 | . B N . B P . .
    2 | P P P Q . . P P
    1 | . . K R . . . R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

Jawab:

✓ White king ke saamne pawns todne aur e4 par dabaav ke liye

-   Rook dekar knight liya: White ki queenside pawn structure tooti aur Black
    ko attack mila.
-   Material kam, par position behtar.

    9.4.4 Sawal 3: Exchange sacrifice mein tum kya dete ho aur kya lete ho?
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Exchange sacrifice mein tum kya dete ho aur kya lete ho?

Jawab:

✓ Rook (5) dekar minor piece (3)

-   2 point material kam, par badle mein position (strong piece, kamzor king,
    pawn structure).

    9.4.5 Sawal 4: Gambit (pawn sacrifice) kab theek hai?
    -----------------------------------------------------

Gambit (pawn sacrifice) kab theek hai?

Jawab:

✓ Jab badle mein development, center ya attack mile

-   Pawn dekar time (tempo), open lines ya center lena.
-   Agar badle mein kuch na mile to bas pawn kam hai.

    ######################################################################
    ##  LEVEL 10: OPENINGS 1: IDEAS AUR E4 OPENINGS
    ######################################################################

Opening ke ideas, systems, Italian, Ruy Lopez, Sicilian, French aur Caro-Kann

-   Opening principles (Level 4) aur middlegame ke plans ke baad ab asli
    openings: naam, main ideas aur pehli 6-10 chaalein.
-   Har line board par khud khelo.

    10.1 Opening ke ideas
    =====================

Center, development, king safety

Yaad rakho

-   Har opening ke 3 kaam: center, development, king safety.
-   Open games (1.e4 e5) tactical, semi-open (Sicilian/French/Caro-Kann),
    closed (1.d4 d5) positional, Indian (1.d4 Nf6) hypermodern.
-   Openings ratne ki jagah har chaal ka idea samjho.
-   Imbalance: opening asymmetric ho to dono taraf jeetne ke mauke (jaise
    Sicilian).
-   Main line = sabse zyada khela gaya raasta; variation = side raasta. Har ek
    ka naam hota hai.
-   Online opening databases (Lichess/Chess.com explorer): masters ki asli
    stats dekho, apni pasand ka bias kam karo.
-   System = har jawab ke against lagbhag same setup (London, Colle, KIA): kam
    theory, par opponent ko kam pareshani.

Is lesson ke topics

-   10.1.1 Teen kaam
-   10.1.2 Sawal 1: 1.e4 e5 2.Nf3 ke baad Black ki sabse natural chaal?
-   10.1.3 Imbalances, main lines, variations
-   10.1.4 Sawal 2: Sicilian 1.e4 c5 "imbalanced" kyun kehlaati hai?
-   10.1.5 Sawal 3: Nayi opening seekhne se pehle stats kahan dekho?
-   10.1.6 Sawal 4: "Main line" aur "sideline" mein kya farq hai?

    10.1.1 Teen kaam
    ----------------

-   Har opening teen kaam karti hai:
    -   Center control (pawns aur pieces se)
    -   Development (saare minor pieces bahar)
    -   King safety (castling)
-   Openings do family mein aati hain:
    -   Open games 1.e4 e5: tez, tactical.
    -   Semi-open 1.e4 ke against c5, e6, c6: Sicilian, French, Caro-Kann.
    -   Closed 1.d4 d5: positional, plans lambe.
    -   Indian 1.d4 Nf6: flexible, hypermodern (pieces se center control).

    5 | . . b . p . . .
    4 | . . B . P . . .
    3 | . . . P . N . .
    2 | P P P . . P P P
    1 | R N B Q K . . R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

Jawab:

✓ Nc6

-   Nc6 develop karta hai aur e5 ko protect.

    10.1.3 Imbalances, main lines, variations
    -----------------------------------------

-   Imbalance: dono side alag cheezein lein (ek ke paas center, doosre ke paas
    bishop pair). Imbalance = jeetne ke mauke.
-   Main line: sabse popular raasta. Variation: side raasta. Har ek ka naam
    hota hai (Najdorf, Winawer...).
-   Opening databases (Lichess Opening Explorer, Chess.com): masters aur aam
    players ki asli stats. Apni "pasand" se zyada data par bharosa karo.

    10.1.4 Sawal 2: Sicilian 1.e4 c5 "imbalanced" kyun kehlaati hai?
    ----------------------------------------------------------------

Sicilian 1.e4 c5 "imbalanced" kyun kehlaati hai?

Jawab:

✓ Black ka pawn side se aata hai, structure alag-alag banti hai

-   Alag structures = dono ke liye jeetne ke mauke.

    10.1.5 Sawal 3: Nayi opening seekhne se pehle stats kahan dekho?
    ----------------------------------------------------------------

Nayi opening seekhne se pehle stats kahan dekho?

Jawab:

✓ Online opening database / explorer

-   Database masters ke hazaaron games dikhata hai.

    10.1.6 Sawal 4: "Main line" aur "sideline" mein kya farq hai?
    -------------------------------------------------------------

"Main line" aur "sideline" mein kya farq hai?

Jawab:

✓ Main line sabse popular aur theory wali line, sideline kam khele jaane wali

-   Sideline galat nahi hoti, bas kam popular.
-   Beginner ko pehle main ideas samajhne chahiye, poori theory nahi.

    10.2 Opening systems
    ====================

London, Colle, Torre, KIA, English Botvinnik, Benko, Nimzo-Larsen, Van Geet

Yaad rakho

-   System = ek hi setup almost har jawab ke against. Fayda: kam theory.
    Nuksaan: opponent ko kam pareshaani, predictable.
-   London: d4, Bf4, e3, c3, Nf3, Bd3. Colle: d4, Nf3, e3, Bd3, c3 (bishop
    andar).
-   Torre: d4, Nf3, Bg5. KIA (King's Indian Attack): Nf3, g3, Bg2, O-O, d3,
    e4.
-   English Botvinnik: c4, Nc3, g3, Bg2, e4 (center ka "pinja"). Benko's
    opening: 1.g3. Nimzo-Larsen: 1.b3. Van Geet: 1.Nc3.

Is lesson ke topics

-   10.2.1 System kya hai?
-   10.2.2 London System
-   10.2.3 Colle System
-   10.2.4 Torre Attack
-   10.2.5 King's Indian Attack (KIA)
-   10.2.6 English: Botvinnik system
-   10.2.7 Benko's Opening, Nimzo-Larsen, Van Geet
-   10.2.8 Sawal 1: Opening system ka sabse bada fayda?
-   10.2.9 Sawal 2: 1.b3 kis naam se jaana jaata hai?

    10.2.1 System kya hai?
    ----------------------

-   System = ek setup jo tum lagbhag har jawab ke against khelte ho.
-   Pehli 6-8 chaalein yaad, baaki plans.
-   Fayda: kam theory, jaane-pehchaane plans, time bachta hai.
-   Nuksaan: predictable, opponent ko aasaani se barabari mil sakti hai, aur
    kabhi exact jawab zaroori hota hai.

    10.2.2 London System
    --------------------

-   Ek "system": lagbhag har jawab ke against same setup.
-   Club players mein bahut popular.

1. d4 d5 2. Bf4 Nf6 3. e3 e6 4. Nf3 c5 5. c3 Nc6 6. Nbd2 Bd6 7. Bg3 O-O 8. Bd3

-   2.Bf4 bishop pawn chain ke bahar, pehle.
-   3.e3
-   5.c3 triangle: c3-d4-e3 solid center.
-   6…Bd6 Black ...Bd6 se bishop trade maangta hai.
-   7.Bg3 bishop bacha, h-file khul sakti hai.
-   8.Bd3 Plan: O-O, Ne5, f4, kingside attack.

-   5.c3 "Colle triangle".

    10.2.5 King's Indian Attack (KIA)
    ---------------------------------

-   King's Indian ka ulta setup White se.
-   Fischer ki favourite.

1. Nf3 d5 2. g3 Nf6 3. Bg2 e6 4. O-O Be7 5. d3 O-O 6. Nbd2 c5 7. e4

-   2.g3 fianchetto.
-   4.O-O Jaldi castle.
-   5.d3
-   7.e4 center break. Baad mein e5 aur kingside attack.

    10.2.6 English: Botvinnik system
    --------------------------------

-   Pawns c4 aur e4 se d5 square par kabza.

1. c4 e5 2. Nc3 Nc6 3. g3 g6 4. Bg2 Bg7 5. e4 d6 6. Nge2

-   1.c4 English.
-   3.g3 fianchetto.
-   5.e4! Botvinnik setup: d5 par pakad.
-   6.Nge2 knight e2 se, f-pawn free.

    10.2.7 Benko's Opening, Nimzo-Larsen, Van Geet
    ----------------------------------------------

-   Kam khele jaane wale "surprise" systems:
    -   Benko's Opening 1.g3: fianchetto, flexible.
    -   Nimzo-Larsen 1.b3: bishop b2 se long diagonal.
    -   Van Geet 1.Nc3: knight pehle (bahut naam hain: Dunst, Sleipner...).
-   Yahan 1.g3 dekho.

1. g3 d5 2. Bg2 e5

-   1.g3
-   2.Bg2 bishop long diagonal par: e4, d5, b7 par nazar.

    8 | r n b q k b n r
    7 | p p p . . p p p

    10.2.8 Sawal 1: Opening system ka sabse bada fayda?
    ---------------------------------------------------

Opening system ka sabse bada fayda?

Jawab:

✓ Kam theory: same setup har jawab ke against

-   System = kam yaad karna, plans pe dhyan.

    10.2.9 Sawal 2: 1.b3 kis naam se jaana jaata hai?
    -------------------------------------------------

1.b3 kis naam se jaana jaata hai?

Jawab:

✓ Nimzo-Larsen Attack

-   Larsen ka favourite.

    10.3 Italian Game
    =================

1.e4 e5 2.Nf3 Nc6 3.Bc4

Yaad rakho

-   1.e4 e5 2.Nf3 Nc6 3.Bc4: bishop kamzor f7 ko dekhta hai.
-   Giuoco Piano plan: c3 + d3, castle, Re1, Bb3, phir d4 break.
-   Two Knights mein 4.Ng5 d5! 5.exd5 Na5! sahi. 5...Nxd5? par Fried Liver
    6.Nxf7!.
-   Two Knights mein 5...Nxd5? par 6.Nxf7! = Fried Liver attack. Sahi:
    5...Na5.
-   Italian ke traps: f7 par Ng5 + Bc4, aur opponent ke jaldi galtiyon ko
    saza.

Is lesson ke topics

-   10.3.1 Italian Game (Giuoco Piano)
-   10.3.2 Two Knights: Fried Liver ka khatra
-   10.3.3 Sawal 1: Italian Game mein White ka bishop c4 kis kamzor square ko
    target…
-   10.3.4 Fried Liver Attack
-   10.3.5 Abhyaas: Two Knights

    10.3.1 Italian Game (Giuoco Piano)
    ----------------------------------

-   Beginners ke liye sabse achhi opening: simple development aur saaf plans.

1. e4 e5 2. Nf3 Nc6 3. Bc4 Bc5 4. c3 Nf6 5. d3 d6 6. O-O O-O 7. Re1 a6 8. Bb3
Ba7

-   1.e4 Center mein pawn aur bishop/queen ke liye raasta.
-   2.Nf3 Knight develop, e5 par attack.
-   3.Bc4 bishop f7 (kamzor square) ko dekhta hai.
-   3…Bc5 Black bhi bishop c5 par: f2 ko dekhta hai.
-   4.c3 idea: baad mein d4 se center.
-   5.d3 shaant aur solid (Giuoco Pianissimo).
-   6.O-O Castle! King safe.
-   Re1 rook ko center file par.
-   Bb3 bishop ko ...Na5 ya ...d5 se bachaya.
-   8…Ba7 Dono taraf development poora. Plan: White Nbd2-f1-g3, h3, aur d4 ka
    break.

    8 | r . b q k . n r
    7 | p p p p . p p p
    6 | . . n . . . . .
    5 | . . b . p . . .
    4 | . . B . P . . .
    3 | . . P . . N . .
    2 | P P . P . P P P
    1 | R N B Q K . . R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

        a b c d e f g h

    10.3.2 Two Knights: Fried Liver ka khatra
    -----------------------------------------

-   3...Nf6 ke baad White 4.Ng5 se f7 par attack karta hai.

1. e4 e5 2. Nf3 Nc6 3. Bc4 Nf6 4. Ng5 d5 5. exd5 Na5

-   4.Ng5 f7 par do attackers.
-   4...d5! sahi jawab: bishop ka raasta band.
-   5.exd5
-   5...Na5! Sahi! Agar 5...Nxd5? to 6.Nxf7! (Fried Liver): king bahar aata
    hai aur bada attack.

    10.3.3 Sawal 1: Italian Game mein White ka bishop c4 kis kamzor square ko target…
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Jawab:

✓ f7

-   f7 shuru mein sirf king protect karta hai.

    10.3.4 Fried Liver Attack
    -------------------------

-   Agar Black 5...Nxd5? khele to White knight sacrifice karta hai.

1. e4 e5 2. Nf3 Nc6 3. Bc4 Nf6 4. Ng5 d5 5. exd5 Nxd5 6. Nxf7 Kxf7 7. Qf3+ Ke6
8. Nc3

-   5...Nxd5? galti.
-   6.Nxf7! knight sacrifice: king aur queen par fork.
-   6...Kxf7
-   7.Qf3+ king aur d5 knight par attack.
-   7...Ke6 king center mein!
-   8.Nc3 aur pin par aur attackers. White ka bada attack.

    8 | r . b q k b . r
    7 | p p p . . p p p
    6 | . . n . . . . .
    5 | . . . n p . N .
    4 | . . B . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | P P P P . P P P
    1 | R N B Q K . . R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    10.3.5 Abhyaas: Two Knights
    ---------------------------

Two Knights: White ne 4.Ng5 khela, phir 5.exd5. Black ki sabse sahi chaal?

    2 | P P P . P P P P

        h g f e d c b a

(Black ki chaal)

Jawab:

1… Na5

Ye bhi sahi: b5 Nd4

-   5...Na5! bishop c4 par attack, Nxd5? ke Fried Liver se bachao.

    1 | R . . K Q B N R
    2 | P P P . P P P P

Chaal ke baad

    10.4 Ruy Lopez (Spanish)
    ========================

1.e4 e5 2.Nf3 Nc6 3.Bb5

Yaad rakho

-   3.Bb5: e5 ke defender c6 knight par pressure.
-   Morphy Defense 3...a6, White Ba4, O-O, Re1, c3, h3.
-   h3 = prophylaxis: ...Bg4 pin roka. Plan d4 aur Nbd2-f1-g3.
-   Black ki 3rd chaal ke options: 3...a6 (Morphy, main), 3...Nf6 (Berlin),
    3...d6 (Steinitz), 3...f5 (Schliemann), 3...Bc5 (Classical), 3...Nd4
    (Bird).
-   Exchange variation 4.Bxc6: Black ko doubled pawns, par bishop pair.

Is lesson ke topics

-   10.4.1 Ruy Lopez: main line
-   10.4.2 Sawal 1: Ruy Lopez mein 3.Bb5 ka main idea?
-   10.4.3 Ruy Lopez: Black ke 3rd move options
-   10.4.4 Sawal 2: Ruy Lopez mein 3...Nf6 ka naam?
-   10.4.5 Exchange Variation

    10.4.1 Ruy Lopez: main line
    ---------------------------

-   World Championship level ki opening. 500 saal purani, aaj bhi sabse
    popular.

1. e4 e5 2. Nf3 Nc6 3. Bb5 a6 4. Ba4 Nf6 5. O-O Be7 6. Re1 b5 7. Bb3 d6 8. c3
O-O 9. h3

-   3.Bb5 e5 ke defender (c6 knight) par pressure.
-   3...a6 bishop se seedha sawaal (Morphy Defense).
-   4.Ba4 bishop pressure banaye rakhta hai.
-   5.O-O castle pehle. e4 pawn ka khatra nahi kyunki baad mein Re1.
-   6.Re1 e4 protect.
-   6...b5 bishop ko door kiya.
-   8.c3 taaki d4 khel sakein aur bishop ke liye c2 ka ghar.
-   9.h3 ...Bg4 pin rokta hai. Plan: d4, Nbd2-f1-g3.

    8 | r . b q k b . r
    7 | . p p p . p p p

    10.4.3 Ruy Lopez: Black ke 3rd move options
    -------------------------------------------

-   1.e4 e5 2.Nf3 Nc6 3.Bb5 ke baad:
    -   3...a6 Morphy Defense (main line).
    -   3...Nf6 Berlin (bahut solid, Kramnik ne Kasparov ke against 2000 mein
        use kiya).
    -   3...d6 Steinitz, 3...Bc5 Classical, 3...f5 Schliemann (gambit),
        3...Nd4 Bird.
    -   4.Bxc6 Exchange variation: Black ke doubled pawns, par bishop pair.

    10.4.4 Sawal 2: Ruy Lopez mein 3...Nf6 ka naam?
    -----------------------------------------------

Ruy Lopez mein 3...Nf6 ka naam?

✓ Berlin Defense

-   Berlin: "Berlin Wall".

    10.4.5 Exchange Variation

-   4.Bxc6 exchange.
-   4...dxc6 Black ke c-pawns doubled, par bishops dono.
-   6.d4 center kholo. Endgame mein White ka 4 vs 3 kingside majority strong.

    10.5 Sicilian Defense
    =====================

1.e4 c5: sabse fighting jawab

Yaad rakho

-   1...c5: side se center par attack, asymmetric position = jeet ke chances.
-   Open Sicilian: 2.Nf3 3.d4 cxd4 4.Nxd4. Black ko extra center pawn milta
    hai.
-   Najdorf 5...a6. English Attack (Be3, f3, Qd2, O-O-O, g4) = opposite
    castling attack race.
-   2.Nf3 ke baad Black: 2...d6 (Najdorf/Dragon), 2...e6
    (Taimanov/Kan/Scheveningen), 2...Nc6 (Classical, Sveshnikov).
-   White ke anti-Sicilians: 2.c3 Alapin, 2.Nc3 Closed, Grand Prix (f4),
    Smith-Morra gambit (2.d4 cxd4 3.c3).

Is lesson ke topics

-   10.5.1 Open Sicilian: Najdorf
-   10.5.2 Sawal 1: Sicilian (1...c5) kyun popular hai?
-   10.5.3 Sicilian ke parivar
-   10.5.4 Alapin Sicilian (2.c3)
-   10.5.5 Sawal 2: 1.e4 c5 2.Nf3 d6 3.d4 cxd4 4.Nxd4 Nf6 5.Nc3 g6 ka naam?

    10.5.1 Open Sicilian: Najdorf
    -----------------------------

-   Sicilian 1.e4 ke against sabse popular aur jeetne wala jawab.
-   Kasparov aur Fischer ki favourite.

1. e4 c5 2. Nf3 d6 3. d4 cxd4 4. Nxd4 Nf6 5. Nc3 a6 6. Be3 e5 7. Nb3 Be6 8. f3

-   1...c5 side se center par attack. Position asymmetric = jeet ke chances.
-   3.d4 White center kholta hai.
-   3...cxd4 Black ko extra center pawn mila (d-pawn).
-   4...Nf6 e4 par attack.
-   5...a6 Najdorf! b5 square control aur flexible.
-   6...e5 center mein space, knight ko bhagaya.
-   7...Be6 development.
-   8.f3 English Attack: f3, Qd2, O-O-O, g4. Dono taraf opposite castling =
    tez attack race.

    gambit.

    10.6 French aur Caro-Kann
    =========================

1.e4 e6 aur 1.e4 c6: solid defenses

Yaad rakho

-   French 1...e6 2...d5: pawn chain d4-e5 banti hai, Black ...c5 se base par
    vaar karta hai.
-   French ki kamzori: c8 bishop band (bad bishop).
-   Caro-Kann 1...c6 2...d5: solid, aur light bishop ...Bf5 se bahar aata hai.
-   French ke variations: Advance (3.e5), Winawer (3.Nc3 Bb4), Tarrasch
    (3.Nd2), Exchange (3.exd5).
-   Caro-Kann ke variations: Classical (4...Bf5), Advance (3.e5), Exchange,
    Panov (c4).
-   French Advance trap: 6...cxd4 7.cxd4 Nxd4?? 8.Nxd4 Qxd4 9.Bb5+ (discovered
    attack, queen gayi).

Is lesson ke topics

    10.6.1 French Defense
    ---------------------

-   Solid aur counter-attacking.
-   Pawn chain samajhna zaroori.

1. e4 e6 2. d4 d5 3. Nc3 Nf6 4. e5 Nfd7 5. f4 c5 6. Nf3 Nc6 7. Be3

-   1...e6 taiyaari d5 ki.
-   2...d5 center ko challenge.
-   4.e5 space! Ab pawn chain bani: d4-e5.
-   5.f4 chain ko mazboot.
-   5...c5! chain ki base (d4) par attack: French ka main idea.
-   7.Be3 Black ki problem: c8 bishop band hai (bad bishop). Plan: ...Qb6,
    ...cxd4, ...f6.

    10.7 Italian Game (club): Giuoco Piano, Two Knights, sidelines
    ==============================================================

Main line 10+ chaal, Black ki galat chaalon ko saza, dono taraf ke traps

Yaad rakho

-   Giuoco Pianissimo: c3, d3, O-O, Re1, a4, h3, Nbd2-f1-g3. Dheema plan, phir
    d4 ya kingside.
-   Two Knights 4.Ng5 d5 5.exd5 Na5!: Black pawn deta hai par tez development.
    5...Nxd5? = Fried Liver.
-   Two Knights 4.d3: shaant line, Giuoco Pianissimo jaisi (transposition).
-   Blackburne Shilling 3...Nd4?!: 4.Nxe5? mat lo (4...Qg5!). 4.Nxd4 ya 4.O-O
    seedha.
-   Black ka passive 3...d6/3...Be7 (Hungarian): White d4 se center le.

    10.8 Ruy Lopez (club): Closed, Berlin, Marshall, Exchange
    =========================================================

Main lines 12+ chaal aur Noah's Ark trap

Yaad rakho

-   Closed Ruy: ...Be7, ...b5, ...d6, ...O-O, c3, h3. Chigorin ...Na5/...c5,
    Breyer ...Nb8-d7, Zaitsev ...Bb7/...Re8.
-   Berlin 3...Nf6 4.O-O Nxe4 5.d4 Nd6 6.Bxc6 dxc6 7.dxe5 Nf5 8.Qxd8+ Kxd8:
    queens jaldi trade, Black solid.
-   Marshall 8...d5: Black pawn de kar White king par attack. 12.d4 Bd6 13.Re1
    Qh4 14.g3 Qh3.
-   Exchange 4.Bxc6 dxc6: White ka plan pawn endgame (4 vs 3 kingside). Black
    ko 2 bishops.
-   Noah's Ark: ...b5, ...c5, ...c4 se bishop b3 phansta hai. White Qxd4?? mat
    khelo.

    10.8.4 Sawal 1: Berlin endgame mein Black king d8/e8 par hai aur castle nahi kar sakta
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Berlin endgame mein Black king d8/e8 par hai aur castle nahi kar sakta. Kya
yeh problem hai?

Jawab:

✓ Nahi: queens trade ho gayi, aur Black ko 2 bishops mile

-   Queens nahi to king par mate ka khatra kam.
-   Black ke 2 bishops lambe samay mein strong hain.

    --------------------------------------------------------------

Jawab:

-   White ka 4 vs 3 kingside majority bana sakta hai.

    10.9 Scotch, Four Knights, Ponziani, Center Game (club)
    =======================================================

White ke seedhe center openings aur Black ke best jawab

-   Scotch Classical 4...Bc5 5.Be3 Qf6 6.c3 Nge7: d4 knight par dabaav. Mieses
    4...Nf6 5.Nxc6 bxc6 6.e5 Qe7 7.Qe2 Nd5 8.c4.
-   Scotch Gambit 4.Bc4: Two Knights jaisi positions mein transpose karta hai.
-   Four Knights Spanish 4.Bb5: Black ...Nd4! (Rubinstein) ya ...Bb4 mirror.
-   Ponziani 3.c3: Black ...d5 ya ...Nf6 se turant e4 par chot.
-   Center Game 2.d4 exd4 3.Qxd4?!: queen jaldi bahar, 3...Nc6 tempo se.

    10.10 King's Gambit aur Vienna (club): dono taraf
    =================================================

Accepted, Falkbeer, Classical declined, Vienna Gambit, Frankenstein-Dracula

Yaad rakho

-   KGA modern: 3...d5 4.exd5 Nf6: Black pawn wapas deta hai, tez development.
-   KGA 3...g5: Black pawn pakadta hai. White 4.h4 (Kieseritzky) se kingside
    todta hai.
-   Falkbeer 2...d5 3.exd5 exf4: Black counter-gambit, f4 pawn ka sahara.
-   Vienna Gambit 3.f4 d5!: Black ka sahi jawab. 4.fxe5 Nxe4 5.Nf3.
-   Vienna 2...Nf6 3.Bc4 Nxe4!? = Frankenstein-Dracula: bahut tez tactics.

    10.10.9 Sawal 1: King's Gambit mein Black ka sabse simple aur safe tareeka?
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

King's Gambit mein Black ka sabse simple aur safe tareeka?

Jawab:

✓ ...d5 se pawn wapas dena aur develop karna

-   3...d5 (ya Falkbeer 2...d5) se Black development leta hai aur White ka
    attack thanda.

    10.11 Petrov aur Philidor (club): dono taraf
    ============================================

Black ke solid 1...e5 weapons, aur White ke traps

Yaad rakho

-   Petrov main: 3.Nxe5 d6 4.Nf3 Nxe4 5.d4 d5 6.Bd3. 5.Nc3 Nxc3 6.dxc3:
    opposite castling.
-   Petrov mein 3...Nxe4? nahi. Aur 4.Nxf7?! (Cochrane) dikhe to 4...Kxf7 aur
    aaram.
-   Philidor: ...d6, ...Nf6, ...Nbd7, ...Be7, ...O-O, ...c6 (Hanham/Lion). e5
    ko pawn se pakdo.
-   Philidor mein ...Bg4? + Nxe5! = Legal's mate ka khatra.
-   Philidor ke khilaaf White: d4, Nc3, Bc4, phir Ng5/Bxf7+ tricks.

Is lesson ke topics

    10.12 French Defence (club): Advance, Winawer, Classical, Tarrasch, Exchange
    ======================================================================

Har White system ke khilaaf Black ka main line, aur White ke plans

Yaad rakho

-   Advance 3.e5 c5 4.c3 Nc6 5.Nf3 Qb6 6.a3: Black d4 par dabaav, White b4 se
    jagah.
-   Winawer 3.Nc3 Bb4 4.e5 c5 5.a3 Bxc3+ 6.bxc3 Ne7 7.Qg4: White g7 par, Black
    queenside par.
-   Classical 3.Nc3 Nf6 4.Bg5 Be7 5.e5 Nfd7 6.h4 (Alekhine-Chatard) ya
    Steinitz 4.e5 Nfd7 5.f4 c5.
-   Tarrasch 3.Nd2 c5 4.exd5 Qxd5: IQP ya open lines. 3...Nf6 se closed lines.
-   Exchange 3.exd5 exd5: symmetrical, Black ka bishop c8 free: aaram se
    barabar.

    10.13 Caro-Kann (club): Advance, Classical, Panov, Exchange, Two Knights
    ======================================================================

Har system ka main line aur traps

Yaad rakho

-   Advance 3.e5 Bf5 4.Nf3 e6 5.Be2 c5 (Short) ya 4.Nc3 e6 5.g4 (Bayonet):
    bishop ko pareshan karna.
-   Classical 4...Bf5 5.Ng3 Bg6 6.h4 h6 7.Nf3 Nd7 8.h5 Bh7 9.Bd3 Bxd3 10.Qxd3.
-   Panov 3.exd5 cxd5 4.c4: IQP positions. Black ...Nf6, ...e6, ...Be7.
-   Exchange 4.Bd3 Nc6 5.c3: Carlsbad structure (ulta QGD). Black ...Bg4 ya
    ...Qc7.
-   Trap: ...Nd7 + Qe2 par ...Ngf6?? Nd6#. Aur Advance mein ...Bf5 ke baad g4
    se bishop phansta hai.

    10.14 Scandinavian, Pirc/Modern, Alekhine (club): dono taraf
    ============================================================

Qa5, Qd6, Modern Scandi; 150 Attack, Austrian; Four Pawns aur Modern Alekhine

Yaad rakho

-   Scandinavian 3...Qa5: ...Nf6, ...Bf5/...Bg4, ...c6, ...e6. 3...Qd6:
    modern, solid.
-   Modern Scandi 2...Nf6 3.d4 Nxd5 4.c4 Nb6: Black center par baad mein chot.
-   Pirc 150 Attack: Be3, Qd2, f3/Bh6. Austrian: f4 se bada center. Black
    ...c6/...b5 ya ...e5/...c5 break.
-   Alekhine Four Pawns e5 c4 d4 f4: bahut jagah par kamzori. Black ...dxe5,
    ...Bf5, ...Nc6, ...e6.
-   In "hypermodern" defences mein White ka simple rule: center rakho, develop
    karo, zyada aage mat badho.

    ...Nbd7. Opposite castling race.
-   6.Be3 Ng4!? bishop ko pareshan karta hai: 7.Bg5 h6 8.Bh4 g5 9.Bg3 Bg7.
-   6.Bg5 e6 7.f4: sabse tez. 7...Be7 8.Qf3 Qc7 9.O-O-O Nbd7.

-   Najdorf ...e5 ke baad d5 square kamzor: Black use ...Be6 se control kare.

    10.16 Dragon aur Accelerated Dragon (club)

Yaad rakho

-   Yugoslav: Be3, f3, Qd2, O-O-O, Bc4/g4/h4. Plan: Bh6 se dragon bishop
    trade, phir h-file par mate.
-   9.O-O-O ke khilaaf 9...d5! center todo.
-   9.Bc4 Bd7 10.O-O-O Rc8 11.Bb3 Ne5 12.h4 h5 (Soltis): h5 se White ka h-file
    attack dheema.
-   Accelerated Dragon (...g6 jaldi, ...d6 nahi): ...d5 ek chaal mein. White
    c4 (Maroczy Bind) se d5 rokta hai.
-   Maroczy ke khilaaf Black: pieces trade (...Nxd4), ...Bd7-c6, ...a5,
    ...b5/...f5 breaks.

    10.17 Sveshnikov, Taimanov, Kan (club)
    ======================================

...e5 aur ...e6 Sicilians ke main lines

Yaad rakho

-   Sveshnikov main: 6.Ndb5 d6 7.Bg5 a6 8.Na3 b5 9.Bxf6 gxf6 10.Nd5 f5. Black
    ke doubled pawns, par 2 bishops aur ...f5 break.
-   7.Nd5 (Sveshnikov) knight trade se d5 par pakad.
-   Taimanov ...e6, ...Nc6, ...Qc7, ...a6: flexible. English Attack setup ke
    khilaaf bhi.
-   Kan ...e6, ...a6: queen c7, bishop c5/d6/e7. White Bd3, c4 (Maroczy
    jaisa).
-   ...e6 Sicilians mein d6 pawn kamzor ho sakta hai: White Nb5/Ndb5 tricks
    par dhyan.

    10.18 Anti-Sicilians (club): dono taraf
    =======================================

Yaad rakho

-   Alapin 2.c3: 2...d5 3.exd5 Qxd5 4.d4 Nf6 5.Nf3 Bg4 ya 2...Nf6 3.e5 Nd5.
-   Rossolimo 3.Bb5 (2...Nc6 ke baad): 3...g6 4.Bxc6 dxc6 5.d3 Bg7: Black ko 2
    bishops, White ko achha structure.
-   Moscow 3.Bb5+ (2...d6 ke baad): 3...Bd7 4.Bxd7+ Qxd7 5.c4: Maroczy jaisa.
-   Closed 2.Nc3 + g3: Black ...g6, ...Bg7, ...d6, ...e6/...e5, ...Nge7.
-   Smith-Morra Siberian trap: ...Qc7, ...Nf6, ...Ng4. h3?? par ...Nd4!.

    ##  LEVEL 11: OPENINGS 2: D4 OPENINGS, GAMBITS AUR TRAPS

Queen's Gambit, Slav, Indian defenses, English, gambits, advanced traps,

    11.1 Queen's Gambit aur Slav
    ============================

QGD, QGA, Slav

Yaad rakho

-   Queen's Gambit 1.d4 d5 2.c4: pawn asli mein free nahi.
-   QGD 2...e6: solid. White plan minority attack b4-b5.
-   Slav 2...c6: d5 support aur light bishop bahar.
-   London Bf4, e3, c3, Nf3, Nbd2, Bd3: har jawab ke against same setup.
-   Queen's Gambit asli gambit nahi: 2...dxc4 ke baad pawn bachane ki koshish
    Black ki position kharab karti hai.
-   QGA: 2...dxc4 3.e3/e4, White center lekar pawn wapas leta hai.

Is lesson ke topics

-   11.1.1 Queen's Gambit Declined
-   11.1.2 Slav Defense
-   11.1.3 Sawal 1: London System ki khaasiyat?
-   11.1.4 Queen's Gambit Accepted (QGA)
-   11.1.5 Sawal 2: 1.d4 d5 2.c4 dxc4

    11.1.1 Queen's Gambit Declined
    ------------------------------

-   Classical aur strategic.
-   "Gambit" naam hai par pawn asli mein free nahi.

1. d4 d5 2. c4 e6 3. Nc3 Nf6 4. Bg5 Be7 5. e3 O-O 6. Nf3 Nbd7 7. Rc1 c6

-   2.c4 side se d5 par attack. Agar ...dxc4, White aksar pawn wapas le leta
    hai.
-   2...e6 Declined: d5 ko pawn se support.
-   4.Bg5 f6 knight ka pin.
-   5.e3 solid.
-   7.Rc1 c-file par pressure.
-   7…c6 Plans: White minority attack (b4-b5), Black ...dxc4 aur ...e5/...c5
    breaks.

    8 | r n b q k b . r
    7 | p p p . . p p p
    6 | . . . . p n . .

    11.2 Indian defenses aur English
    ================================

King's Indian, Nimzo-Indian, Grunfeld, English

Yaad rakho

-   King's Indian: Black center White ko deta hai, phir ...e5/...f5 se vaar.
    Center band ho to Black kingside, White queenside.
-   Nimzo-Indian 3...Bb4: c3 knight ka pin aur e4 par control.
-   English 1.c4: ulta Sicilian, g3 + Bg2 fianchetto.
-   Grunfeld: Black White ko bada center deta hai, phir ...d5 aur bishop g7 se
    center par hamla.

Is lesson ke topics

-   11.2.1 King's Indian Defense
-   11.2.2 Nimzo-Indian Defense
-   11.2.3 English Opening

    attack…
-   11.2.5 Grunfeld Defense
-   11.2.6 Sawal 2: Grunfeld mein Black ka idea?

    11.2.1 King's Indian Defense
    ----------------------------

-   Black White ko center lene deta hai, phir ...e5/...f5 se attack.
-   Fischer aur Kasparov ki pasand.

1. d4 Nf6 2. c4 g6 3. Nc3 Bg7 4. e4 d6 5. Nf3 O-O 6. Be2 e5 7. O-O Nc6 8. d5
Ne7

-   2...g6 fianchetto ki taiyaari.
-   3...Bg7 bishop long diagonal par.
-   4.e4 White ne bada center liya.
-   5…O-O Pehle castle.
-   6...e5 center par vaar.
-   8.d5 center band. Ab White queenside (c5), Black kingside (...f5, ...g5)
    par attack karega.

    11.3 Gambits
    ============

Kyun gambit, semi-open files, King's, Danish, Evans, Albin, Benko,
Blackmar-Diemer, Smith-Morra, Staunton, Reti gambit

Yaad rakho

-   Gambit = development (tempo) aur open lines ke liye pawn dena.
-   Semi-open (half-open) file: sirf dushman ka pawn. Rook ke liye attack ka
    raasta. Gambits aksar yeh files aur tempo dete hain.
-   White gambits: King's (2.f4), Danish (d4, c3), Evans (b4), Smith-Morra (vs
    Sicilian), Blackmar-Diemer (d4 e4), Staunton (vs Dutch), Reti gambit (b3
    vs French), Queen's Gambit.
-   Black gambits: Albin (2...e5 vs QG), Benko (3...b5).

Is lesson ke topics

-   11.3.1 Gambit kyun?
-   11.3.2 King's Gambit
-   11.3.3 Danish Gambit
-   11.3.4 Evans Gambit
-   11.3.5 Smith-Morra Gambit (vs Sicilian)
-   11.3.6 Blackmar-Diemer Gambit
-   11.3.7 Staunton Gambit (vs Dutch)
-   11.3.8 Reti Gambit (vs French)
-   11.3.9 Albin Counter-Gambit (Black)
-   11.3.10 Benko Gambit (Black)
-   11.3.11 Sawal 1: Gambit dene wale ko badle mein kya chahiye?
-   11.3.12 Sawal 2: Semi-open file kya hai?

    11.3.1 Gambit kyun?
    -------------------

-   Gambit = shuru mein pawn (kabhi piece) dekar badle mein development
    (tempo), open lines aur attack lena.
-   Semi-open (half-open) file: jisme sirf dushman ka pawn ho.
-   Tumhare rook ke liye attack ka raasta.
-   Gambits aksar aisi files aur tempo dete hain.
-   Gambit khelo to tez development aur attack zaroori.
-   Jawab dena ho to: development karo, aur sahi waqt par pawn wapas de do.

    11.3.2 King's Gambit
    --------------------

-   19vi sadi ka sabse romantic gambit.
-   f-file khulti hai.

1. e4 e5 2. f4 exf4 3. Nf3 g5 4. Bc4 Bg7 5. O-O

-   2.f4 King's Gambit.
-   2...exf4 accepted.
-   3...g5 pawn bachane ki koshish.
-   5.O-O aur f-file par rook: f7 par pressure.

    8 | r n b q k . n r
    7 | p p p p . p b p
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . p .
    4 | . . B . P p . .
    3 | . . . . . N . .
    2 | P P P P . . P P
    1 | R N B Q . R K .
       ----------------

    11.3.11 Sawal 1: Gambit dene wale ko badle mein kya chahiye?
    ------------------------------------------------------------

Gambit dene wale ko badle mein kya chahiye?

Jawab:

✓ Tez development (tempo), open lines, attack

-   Material ke badle time aur activity.

    11.3.12 Sawal 2: Semi-open file kya hai?
    ----------------------------------------

Semi-open file kya hai?

Jawab:

✓ Jisme sirf dushman ka pawn hai

-   Rook ke liye attack ka raasta (dushman pawn par).

    11.4 Advanced opening traps
    ===========================

Lasker, Caro-Kann, Three Knights, Petrov copycat, QGA, Cambridge Springs,
Mortimer, Noah's Ark, Fischer, Siberian, Stafford, Vienna

Yaad rakho

-   Opponent koi pawn "free" chhode to pehle socho: trap to nahi?
-   Blackburne Shilling: 3...Nd4 ke baad 4.Nxe5? lalach, ...Qg5! aur smothered
    mate.
-   Sahi jawab: 4.Nxd4 ya 4.O-O.
-   Traps khud na bhi khelo, unke tactical ingredients (pin, fork, discovered
    attack, underpromotion) seekho.
-   Copycat mat bano: opponent ki nakal (Petrov) se trap mein phans sakte ho.
-   Bishop trap (Noah's Ark): ...c5-c4 se bishop b3 band.

Is lesson ke topics

-   11.4.1 Sawal 1: Jab opponent koi pawn "free" chhod de, sabse pehle kya
    socho?
-   11.4.2 Advanced traps: ingredients seekho
-   11.4.3 Albin: Lasker trap (underpromotion!)
-   11.4.4 Caro-Kann trap
-   11.4.5 Three Knights: `Nxe5` trick
-   11.4.6 Petrov: copycat ki saza
-   11.4.7 Queen's Gambit Accepted trap
-   11.4.8 Cambridge Springs trap
-   11.4.9 Ruy Lopez: Mortimer trap
-   11.4.10 Ruy Lopez: Noah's Ark trap
-   11.4.11 Sicilian: Fischer trap (Fischer vs Reshevsky 1958)
-   11.4.12 Smith-Morra: Siberian trap
-   11.4.13 Stafford Gambit trap
-   11.4.14 Vienna Game trap

    11.4.1 Sawal 1: Jab opponent koi pawn "free" chhod de, sabse pehle kya socho?
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Jab opponent koi pawn "free" chhod de, sabse pehle kya socho?

Jawab:

✓ Yeh trap to nahi? Lene ke baad unki CCT kya hai?

    11.4.2 Advanced traps: ingredients seekho
    -----------------------------------------

-   Yeh traps alphabetical order mein, har opening se ek.
-   Agar tum woh opening kabhi na bhi khelo, ingredients seekho: pin, fork,
    discovered attack, underpromotion, trapped piece.
-   Yahi patterns middlegame mein bhi aate hain.

    11.4.3 Albin: Lasker trap (underpromotion!)
    -------------------------------------------

-   Black ka pawn aakhir mein knight banta hai!

1. d4 d5 2. c4 e5 3. dxe5 d4 4. e3 Bb4+ 5. Bd2 dxe3 6. Bxb4 exf2+ 7. Ke2
fxg1=N+

-   4.e3? galti.
-   4...Bb4+
-   5...dxe3!
-   6.Bxb4?
-   6...exf2+
-   7.Ke2
-   7...fxg1=N+! knight promotion check ke saath. 8.Rxg1 Bg4+ aur queen gayi.

    1 | R N B K Q B N R
    2 | P P P . . . P P
    3 | . . . P . . . .
    4 | . . . . p P . .
    5 | . . . P . . . .
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    7 | p p p . . p p p
    8 | r n b k q b n r

        h g f e d c b a

    11.4.4 Caro-Kann trap
    ---------------------

-   Natural lagne wali chaal se smothered mate!

1. e4 c6 2. d4 d5 3. Nc3 dxe4 4. Nxe4 Nd7 5. Qe2 Ngf6 6. Nd6#

-   5.Qe2 trap set: e7 pawn pinned.
-   5...Ngf6??
-   6.Nd6# e7 pawn pinned hai, isliye capture nahi kar sakta. Smothered mate!

    8 | r . b q k b . r
    7 | p p . n p p p p
    6 | . . p N . n . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . P . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | P P P . Q P P P

        a b c d e f g h

    11.4.5 Three Knights: `Nxe5` trick
    ----------------------------------

-   3...Bc5? natural lagta hai par 4.Nxe5! (fork trick) se White center jeetta
    hai.

1. e4 e5 2. Nf3 Nc6 3. Nc3 Bc5 4. Nxe5 Nxe5 5. d4 Bd6 6. dxe5 Bxe5

-   3...Bc5? behtar 3...Nf6.
-   4.Nxe5! piece "sacrifice".
-   5.d4 fork: bishop aur knight. Piece wapas aur bada center.

    11.4.8 Cambridge Springs trap
    -----------------------------

-   Elephant trap se shuru, phir queen a5 se pin.

1. d4 d5 2. c4 e6 3. Nc3 Nf6 4. Bg5 Nbd7 5. e3 c6 6. Nf3 Qa5 7. Bd3 dxc4
8. Bxc4 Ne4

-   4...Nbd7 (elephant trap set).
-   6...Qa5 Cambridge Springs: c3 knight pinned.
-   7.Bd3? common galti.
-   7...dxc4! bishop ko hataya.
-   8...Ne4! c3 par double pressure aur g5 bishop par attack.

    11.4.14 Vienna Game trap
    ------------------------

-   "Frankenstein-Dracula" variation: dono taraf jungli chaalein.

1. e4 e5 2. Nc3 Nf6 3. Bc4 Nxe4 4. Qh5 Nd6 5. Bb3 Nc6 6. Nb5 g6 7. Qf3 f5
8. Qd5 Qe7 9. Nxc7+ Kd8 10. Nxa8

-   3...Nxe4 fork trick.
-   4.Qh5 mate threat f7.
-   7.Qf3 f7 par phir.
-   8.Qd5 mate threat aur c7.
-   9.Nxc7+
-   10.Nxa8 rook jeeta (par Black ke paas compensation hai).

    8 | r n b q k b . r
    7 | p p p p . p p p
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . p . . Q
    4 | . . B . n . . .
    3 | . . N . . . . .
    2 | P P P P . P P P
    1 | R . B . K . N R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

Position after 4.Qh5

    8 | N . b k . b . r
    7 | p p . p q . . p
    6 | . . n n . . p .
    5 | . . . Q p p . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . B . . . . . .
    2 | P P P P . P P P
    1 | R . B . K . N R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

Final position (10.Nxa8)

    11.5 Apna repertoire banao
    ==========================

Kya khelna hai, kaise yaad karna hai

Yaad rakho

-   White: 1.e4 + Italian (ya 1.d4 + London).
-   Black vs 1.e4: 1...e5 ya Caro-Kann. Black vs 1.d4: QGD ya Slav.
-   Pehle 6-8 chaalon ke ideas; har game ke baad opening check karo.

Is lesson ke topics

-   11.5.1 Simple repertoire (beginner se club level)
-   11.5.2 Sawal 1: 1200 rating par opening kaise padhni chahiye?
-   11.5.3 Sawal 2: Repertoire banane ka achha tareeka?
-   11.5.4 Sawal 3: Ek opening mein haar gaye
-   11.5.5 Sawal 4: Opening seekhte waqt sabse zaroori kya hai?

    11.5.1 Simple repertoire (beginner se club level)
    -------------------------------------------------

-   White: 1.e4 aur Italian Game. (Ya 1.d4 + London System agar positional
    pasand ho.)
-   Black vs 1.e4: 1...e5 (open games) ya Caro-Kann (solid).
-   Black vs 1.d4: Queen's Gambit Declined ya Slav.
-   Kaise seekhein:
-   Pehle 6-8 chaalon ke ideas samjho, lambi theory nahi.
-   Har game ke baad dekho kahan opening se bahar aaye, wahan ka sahi move
    note karo.
-   1600+ ke baad dheere-dheere gehri lines aur sideline traps.
-   Master games dekho apni openings ke: plans wahi se aate hain.

    11.5.2 Sawal 1: 1200 rating par opening kaise padhni chahiye?
    -------------------------------------------------------------

1200 rating par opening kaise padhni chahiye?

Jawab:

✓ Ideas aur principles, 6-8 chaal

    11.5.3 Sawal 2: Repertoire banane ka achha tareeka?
    ---------------------------------------------------

Repertoire banane ka achha tareeka?

Jawab:

✓ White ke liye ek opening, Black ke liye 1.e4 aur 1.d4 ke ek-ek jawab

-   Chhota aur fixed repertoire: wahi positions baar-baar aayengi aur unke
    plans samajh aayenge.

    11.5.4 Sawal 3: Ek opening mein haar gaye
    -----------------------------------------

Ek opening mein haar gaye. Kya karein?

Jawab:

✓ Game dekho kahan galti hui aur woh line seekh lo

-   Haar se repertoire mazboot hota hai: galti wali line ko theek karo.

    11.5.5 Sawal 4: Opening seekhte waqt sabse zaroori kya hai?
    -----------------------------------------------------------

Opening seekhte waqt sabse zaroori kya hai?

Jawab:

✓ Har chaal ka idea aur middlegame ka plan samajhna

-   Idea samjhe to opponent ke alag chalne par bhi sahi chaal mil jaati hai.

    11.6 King's Indian aur Grunfeld (club): dono taraf
    ==================================================

KID Classical/Mar del Plata, Samisch, Four Pawns, Fianchetto; Grunfeld
Exchange, Russian

Yaad rakho

-   KID Mar del Plata: 9.Ne1 Nd7 10.Be3 f5 11.f3 f4: Black kingside attack,
    White queenside (c5).
-   Samisch 5.f3: White Be3, Qd2, O-O-O/g4. Black ...c5 ya ...e5,
    ...Nc6+...a6+...Rb8+...b5.
-   Four Pawns f4: Black ...c5 se turant center todo.
-   Grunfeld Exchange: White ka bada center c3+d4+e4. Black ...c5, ...Nc6,
    ...Bg4, ...Qa5 se d4 par.
-   Grunfeld Russian Qb3: Black ...dxc4, phir ...a6/...Nc6/...Bg4 lines.

    11.6.7 Sawal 1: KID Mar del Plata mein dono ke plans?
    -----------------------------------------------------

KID Mar del Plata mein dono ke plans?

Jawab:

✓ Black kingside (...f5-f4-g5), White queenside (c5, b4)

-   Band center (d5 vs e5) = flanks par race.
-   Pawns jahan "point" karte hain, wahan khelo.

    11.7 Nimzo-Indian, QID, Bogo, Catalan (club)
    ============================================

4.e3 Rubinstein, 4.Qc2 Classical, Samisch; QID 4.g3; Catalan open/closed

Yaad rakho

-   Nimzo Rubinstein 4.e3: ...O-O, ...d5, ...c5. Classical 4.Qc2: ...O-O,
    ...d5 ya ...c5.
-   Nimzo ka trade ...Bxc3: White ko 2 bishops, Black ko better structure
    (doubled c pawns).
-   QID 4.g3 Ba6: c4 par chot. 5.b3 Bb4+ 6.Bd2 Be7.
-   Bogo-Indian 3...Bb4+: Nimzo ka "Nf3" version. Simple, solid.
-   Catalan g3 + Bg2: long diagonal se queenside par dabaav. Black ...dxc4
    (open) ya ...Be7, ...c6 (closed).

Is lesson ke topics

    11.7.7 Sawal 1: Nimzo mein ...Bxc3 ke baad kiske paas kya?
    ----------------------------------------------------------

Nimzo mein ...Bxc3 ke baad kiske paas kya?

Jawab:

✓ White: 2 bishops; Black: better pawn structure

-   Black c4 ko target karta hai, White center aur bishops se khelta hai.

    11.8 Benoni, Benko, Budapest, Dutch (club)
    ==========================================

Black ke fighting defences aur White ke best jawab

Yaad rakho

-   Modern Benoni: Black queenside majority (...b5), White center (e5 break,
    f4). Taimanov f4+Bb5+ sabse khatarnaak.
-   Benko Gambit ...b5: pawn ke badle a/b files par long-term dabaav.
-   Budapest ...e5: White Bf4, Nf3, e3, Nbd2/Nc3; Black pawn wapas leta hai.
    Trap: axb4?? par ...Nd3#.
-   Dutch Leningrad (...g6) aur Stonewall (...d5, ...e6, ...c6): e4 square ki
    ladai.
-   Dutch ke khilaaf White: Staunton 2.e4 ya Bg5, Nc3, e4 break.

Is lesson ke topics

-   11.8.1 Modern Benoni: Classical
-   11.8.2 Benko Gambit
-   11.8.3 Budapest: White ka sahi khel
-   11.8.4 Dutch: Stonewall
-   11.8.5 Dutch ke khilaaf: Staunton Gambit
-   11.8.6 Abhyaas: Benoni Taimanov Attack
-   11.8.7 Sawal 1: Benko Gambit mein Black pawn ke badle kya leta hai?

    11.8.7 Sawal 1: Benko Gambit mein Black pawn ke badle kya leta hai?
    -------------------------------------------------------------------

Benko Gambit mein Black pawn ke badle kya leta hai?

Jawab:

✓ Khuli a- aur b-files par rooks/queen ka long-term dabaav

-   Endgame mein bhi Black ka dabaav rehta hai: isliye yeh "positional gambit"
    hai.

    11.9 English, Reti, KIA, Bird, Larsen (club): dono taraf
    ========================================================

Flank openings ke main lines aur Black ke seedhe jawab

Yaad rakho

-   English 1.c4 e5 (Reversed Sicilian): White g3, Bg2, Nc3, d3. Black ...Nc6,
    ...g6 ya ...Nf6, ...d5.
-   Symmetrical English 1...c5: dono fianchetto; break d4/...d5.
-   Reti 1.Nf3 d5 2.c4: Black ...d4 (space), ...e6 (QGD jaisa) ya ...c6 (Slav
    jaisa).
-   KIA: d3, Nd2, g3, Bg2, O-O, e4, phir e5 + kingside. Black queenside
    (...b5, ...c5).
-   Bird 1.f4: Black ...d5, ...Nf6, ...g6 ya From ...e5. Larsen 1.b3: Black
    ...e5 + ...Nc6 + ...d5.

Is lesson ke topics

-   11.9.1 English: Reversed Sicilian
-   11.9.2 English: Botvinnik setup (Black)
-   11.9.3 Symmetrical English
-   11.9.4 Reti: 2...d4
-   11.9.5 KIA ke khilaaf: queenside plan
-   11.9.6 Larsen: 1.b3 e5
-   11.9.7 Abhyaas: Bird Opening (Leningrad setup)
-   11.9.8 Sawal 1: English 1.c4 e5 ko "Reversed Sicilian" kyun kehte hain?

    11.9.1 English: Reversed Sicilian
    ---------------------------------

-   Black ek tempo kam ke saath Sicilian ka White side khel raha hai.

8. d3 O-O 9. a3 Be6 10. b4

    1 | . K R . Q B . R
    2 | P B P P . . . .
    3 | . P N . P N . P
    4 | . . . . . . P .
    5 | . . . p . . . .

    11.9.8 Sawal 1: English 1.c4 e5 ko "Reversed Sicilian" kyun kehte hain?
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

    11.10 QGD aur QGA (club): main lines dono taraf
    ===============================================

Orthodox, Exchange (Carlsbad), Tartakower, Lasker; QGA Classical

Yaad rakho

-   QGD Orthodox: 4.Bg5 Be7 5.e3 O-O 6.Nf3 h6 7.Bh4 b6 (Tartakower) ya 7...Ne4
    (Lasker): trades se aazaadi.
-   QGD Exchange cxd5 exd5: Carlsbad. White minority attack b4-b5, Black
    kingside ...Ne4, ...f5.
-   Black QGD ka sabse bada sawaal: bishop c8. Hal: ...b6+...Bb7, ya ...dxc4 +
    ...e5/...c5.
-   QGA: 3.e4 (center) ya 3.Nf3. 3.e4 e5! ya 3...Nf6, 3...c5 se center par
    chot.
-   Cambridge Springs trap aur Elephant trap: Nxd5?? ke baad ...Nxd5! Bxd8
    Bb4+.

    11.11 Slav aur Semi-Slav (club)
    ===============================

Slav main, Exchange Slav, Meran, Moscow/Anti-Moscow, Chebanenko

Yaad rakho

-   Slav main: 4.Nc3 dxc4 5.a4 Bf5 6.e3 e6 7.Bxc4 Bb4 8.O-O.
-   Exchange Slav cxd5 cxd5: symmetrical. Black copy-cat tricks se bacho.
-   Semi-Slav ...e6 + ...c6: Meran 6.Bd3 dxc4 7.Bxc4 b5. Moscow 5.Bg5 h6.
-   Chebanenko 4...a6: ...b5 ka idea, flexible.
-   Slav ka fayda: bishop c8 ...e6 se pehle bahar.

    11.12 Tarrasch, Chigorin, Albin, Baltic (club)
    ==============================================

Queen's Gambit ke active jawab, aur White ka sahi khel

Yaad rakho

-   Tarrasch ...c5: Black IQP leta hai active pieces ke liye. White g3 + Bg2
    se d5 par dabaav.
-   Chigorin 2...Nc6: pieces se center. White Nc3 + e4 ya cxd5.
-   Albin ...e5 ...d4: White g3, Bg2, Nbd2, Nb3 se d4 pawn jeetne ki koshish.
-   Baltic 2...Bf5: bishop jaldi; White Qb3 se b7 par.
-   Har gambit ke khilaaf: develop, pawn wapas mat do jab tak zaroori na ho,
    aur Qb3 jaisi tricks.

    11.13 Systems (club): London, Jobava, Colle-Zukertort, Torre, BDG
    =================================================================

White ke systems aur Black ke tayyar jawab

Yaad rakho

-   London ke khilaaf: ...c5 + ...Qb6, ya ...Nh5 se bishop trade, ya KID setup
    (...g6, ...d6, ...e5).
-   Jobava London Nc3 + Bf4: Nb5 ka khatra. Black ...a6/...c6 aur ...Bf5.
-   Colle-Zukertort b3, Bb2, Ne5, f4: Black ...c5, ...Nc6, ...Bb7 mirror.
-   Torre Bg5: ...c5, ...Qb6/...h6 se sawaal.
-   BDG ke khilaaf: ...Bf5, ...e6, ...c6 (Teichmann/Euwe): solid, pawn upar.

Is lesson ke topics

-   11.13.1 London: modern main line
-   11.13.2 London ke khilaaf: ...Nh5
-   11.13.3 Jobava London
-   11.13.4 Colle-Zukertort

-   11.13.6 Abhyaas: London: White ne Qb3 khela
-   11.13.7 Sawal 1: London ke khilaaf ...Nh5 ka idea?

    11.13.1 London: modern main line
    --------------------------------

-   Black ...c5 aur ...Qb6 ke baad White ka standard jawab.

    --------------------------------------------------

✓ Bishop f4/g3 ko trade karna (White ka best piece)

    ######################################################################
    ##  LEVEL 12: ENDGAMES 1: KING AUR PAWNS
    ######################################################################

Endgame ke themes, do bishops se mate, opposition, square rule, triangulation

-   Endgame mein king ladta hai aur pawns queen bante hain.
-   Pehle bade themes aur king-pawn endings.

    12.1 Endgame ke bade themes
    ===========================

Active king, passed pawn ka safar, rook 7th rank par

Yaad rakho

-   Endgame mein king ko center aur aage lao: mate ka khatra kam, king strong
    piece.
-   Passed pawn ko safe raasta do: king ya rook se escort, blockade todo.
-   Rook 7th rank par: pawns aur king dono atakte hain
    (Capablanca-Tartakower).
-   Famous: Tal-Lisitsin (aggressive king), Seirawan-Kasparov (passed pawn ka
    safar).

Is lesson ke topics

-   12.1.1 Endgame ke 3 bade themes
-   12.1.2 Khud khelo: Pawn ko queen banao
-   12.1.3 Abhyaas: Rook ko 7th rank par lao
-   12.1.4 Sawal 1: Endgame mein king kahan hona chahiye?
-   12.1.5 Sawal 2: Endgame mein "passed pawn" ke saath kya karo?

    12.1.1 Endgame ke 3 bade themes
    -------------------------------

-   Aggressive king: endgame mein king center aur aage jaata hai, pawns khaata
    hai, apne pawns ko support karta hai. (Tal vs Lisitsin)
-   Passed pawn ka safe safar: passed pawn ko escort karo (king/rook se),
    blockader ko hatao. (Seirawan vs Kasparov)
-   Rook on the 7th: rook dushman ki 7th rank par: pawns par attack aur king
    band. (Capablanca vs Tartakower, 1924)

    12.1.2 Khud khelo: Pawn ko queen banao
    --------------------------------------

King ko active karo: pawn ko escort karke queen banao (20 chaal).

Hint: Pehle king aage (pawn se aage), opposition lo, phir pawn.

    4 | . . . . . . . .

    1 | . . . . . . . .

        a b c d e f g h

Chaal ke baad

    12.1.4 Sawal 1: Endgame mein king kahan hona chahiye?
    -----------------------------------------------------

Endgame mein king kahan hona chahiye?

Jawab:

✓ Center mein, active

-   Endgame = king attack karta hai.

    12.1.5 Sawal 2: Endgame mein "passed pawn" ke saath kya karo?
    -------------------------------------------------------------

Endgame mein "passed pawn" ke saath kya karo?

Jawab:

✓ Aage badhao: "passed pawns must be pushed"

-   Passed pawn ko koi pawn nahi rok sakta.
-   Aage badhaoge to opponent ke pieces use rokne mein bandh jaate hain.

    12.2 Do bishops se mate
    =======================

K+B+B vs K, endgame mates ka revision

Yaad rakho

-   Do bishops saath-saath diagonals par ek "deewar" banate hain.
-   King ko kone (corner) mein dhakelo; apna king saath laao.
-   Stalemate ka dhyan: king ko hamesha ek chaal chhodo jab tak mate na ho.
-   Revision: Q, R, do rooks, Q+R ke mates Level 2 mein; back-rank mate
    Checkmate patterns mein.

Is lesson ke topics

-   12.2.1 Do bishops se mate
-   12.2.2 Abhyaas: Do bishops
-   12.2.3 Khud khelo: Checkmate karo
-   12.2.4 Sawal 1: Do bishops se mate kahan hota hai?
-   12.2.5 Sawal 2: Agar dono bishops ek hi rang ke squares par hon?

    12.2.1 Do bishops se mate
    -------------------------

-   Do bishops paas-paas diagonals par milkar ek deewar banate hain jo king ko
    kaat deti hai.
-   1.
-   Bishops center ke paas, king ko ek kone ki taraf dhakelo.
-   2.
-   Apna king saath lao: bina king ke mate nahi hota.
-   3.
-   King kone mein aaye to bishops se check aur band.
-   4.
-   Stalemate ka dhyan: har chaal par dekho ki king ke paas chaal bachi hai.
-   Yaad: queen, rook, do rooks, queen+rook ke mates Level 3 mein seekhe.

    12.2.2 Abhyaas: Do bishops
    --------------------------
