Final position (7…Be7)

    18.4 Apni games se repertoire update
    ====================================

Book kahan khatam hua, kisne naya khela, aur review cards: Road ka "Opening
check"

Yaad rakho

-   Har game ke baad: opening book kahan khatam hua? Pehle kisne chhoda?
-   Agar tumne chhoda: kya woh chaal achhi thi? Nahi to sahi line repertoire
    mein jodo.
-   Agar opponent ne chhoda: uska best jawab dhundo (engine game ke BAAD) aur
    yaad karo.
-   Road to 3000 > My Games > Opening check: yeh sab apne aap karta hai aur
    review cards banata hai.
-   Har mahine repertoire report: kis opening mein score kharab, kahan book
    jaldi khatam.

Is lesson ke topics

-   18.4.1 Masters ka workflow
-   18.4.2 Sawal 1: Game mein opponent ne move 6 par book chhod di (naya
    khela)
-   18.4.3 Abhyaas: Tumhari game
-   18.4.4 Abhyaas: Tumhari game
-   18.4.5 Abhyaas: Tumhari game (Black)
-   18.4.6 Sawal 2: Repertoire report mein "Book tak

    18.4.1 Masters ka workflow
    --------------------------

-   1.
-   Game khelo. Opening mein engine nahi!
-   2.
-   Game ke baad: dekho book kahan khatam hua aur kisne pehle naya khela.
-   3.
-   Engine se check: woh chaal achhi thi ya nahi, aur best kya tha.
-   4.
-   Repertoire update: sahi line apni file mein jodo, aur use drill karo
    (spaced review).
-   5.
-   Har mahine report: kis opening mein haar zyada, kahan galtiyan.
-   Is app mein: Road to 3000 > My Games > Opening check.
-   Woh tumhari Lichess games scan karta hai, har game mein book ka end aur
    engine ki best chaal dikhata hai, aur galat chaalon ke review cards banata
    hai jo Course aur Road dono ke review mein aate hain.

    18.4.2 Sawal 1: Game mein opponent ne move 6 par book chhod di (naya khela)
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Game mein opponent ne move 6 par book chhod di (naya khela). Tum kya karoge
game ke baad?

Jawab:

✓ Engine se uska best jawab dekho aur apne repertoire mein jodo

-   Agli baar wahi position aaye to tayyar raho.
-   Yahi repertoire update hai.

    18.4.6 Sawal 2: Repertoire report mein "Book tak
    ------------------------------------------------

Repertoire report mein "Book tak: 4 chaal" aur "Galti: 3" ek opening mein.
Matlab?

Jawab:

✓ Us opening ki theory kam aati hai: uska ladder lesson dobara karo

-   Jaldi book khatam + galtiyan = kamzor area.
-   Seedha usi par kaam karo.

    18.5 Opponent ke liye taiyaari aur final exam
    =============================================

Tournament prep ka tareeka, phir poori ladder ka mixed exam

Yaad rakho

-   Prep: opponent ki games (Lichess) se uski openings dekho, apne repertoire
    mein se sabse achha jawab chuno.
-   Surprise se pehle apni comfort: aisi line chuno jiske plans tum jaante ho.
-   Game se pehle 10 minute: chuni hui line 2 baar drill karo, typical plans
    yaad karo.
-   Novelty mile to ghabrao mat: principles aur structure ke plans.
-   Mastery = theory + plans + update ka cycle, roz.

Is lesson ke topics

-   18.5.1 Tournament prep: 4 kadam
-   18.5.2 Sawal 1: Opponent hamesha London khelta hai
-   18.5.3 Abhyaas: Final exam
-   18.5.4 Abhyaas: Final exam
-   18.5.5 Abhyaas: Final exam
-   18.5.6 Abhyaas: Final exam
-   18.5.7 Abhyaas: Final exam
-   18.5.8 Abhyaas: Final exam
-   18.5.9 Abhyaas: Final exam
-   18.5.10 Abhyaas: Final exam

    18.5.1 Tournament prep: 4 kadam
    -------------------------------

-   1.
-   Opponent ki games (Lichess profile, "Opening explorer" mein uska naam):
    woh White aur Black mein kya khelta hai?
-   2.
-   Apne repertoire se line chuno: uski favourite line ke khilaaf tumhara best
    jawab.
-   3.
-   Gehraai: us line ke plans aur 2-3 sidelines dekho.
-   4.
-   Drill: game se pehle line 2 baar khud khelo.
-   Yaad rakho: comfort zone wali line > ajeeb surprise jiske plans nahi pata.

    18.5.2 Sawal 1: Opponent hamesha London khelta hai
    --------------------------------------------------

Opponent hamesha London khelta hai. Tumhara prep?

Jawab:

✓ Apne repertoire ka London plan (...c5 + ...Qb6 ya ...Nh5) dobara drill karo

-   Jo jaante ho usse pakka karo.
-   Plan ready = confidence.
